It matches neither the prior reversion nor the case-based clamping the literature anticipates, because the reversion target is seed-conditional rather than a single global default, and a leave-one-out selection rule rejects both; the in-range faithfulness slope is statistically indistinguishable from zero wherever a response is estimable at all. A benchmark averaging over seeds would never see this: each sample is confidently locked to a wrong constant, exactly the failure a plausibility score is structurally blind to. We release the protocol, suite, and analysis code.

</details>


### [68] [From Gaze to Meaning: A Training-Free AI Agent for Unified Grounding and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06208)
*Shayan Nasiriboukani,Sara Atito,Mohammad Nezamipour,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出无需训练的人眼注视目标代理（GTA），借助预训练视觉语言模型实现注视引导推理，在GazeFollow和GazeHOI基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景理解中的注意力分析方法依赖大量训练且缺乏可解释性，难以在无监督情况下联合推理注视目标、关注对象和视觉定位。

Method: 利用预训练视觉语言模型，通过视觉引导提示进行增强，并对高不确定样本采用基于记忆的检索策略来提升性能，全程无需额外训练。

Result: 在GazeFollow和GazeHOI基准上定量达到最先进水平；定性上能提供详细语义预测，即使真实标签错误时也能正确预测目标，且无词汇约束限制。

Conclusion: 该训练免费方法在注视目标预测、注意力定位和物体识别等任务上表现出色，兼具可解释性和灵活性。

Abstract: Understanding human attention is fundamental for scene interpretation, yet existing approaches often rely on heavily trained models that lack interpretability. Prior methods struggle to jointly reason about gaze targets, attended objects, and visual grounding without extensive supervision. To the best of our knowledge, this work introduces the first training-free Gaze Target Agent (GTA) for gaze-guided reasoning across tasks such as gaze target prediction, attention localization, and object identification. This is achieved by leveraging pretrained vision-language models, augmenting them with visually guided prompts, and employing a memory-based retrieval strategy for high-uncertainty samples to improve performance without additional training. We evaluate our approach using both quantitative metrics and qualitative results. Quantitatively, our method achieves state of the art performance on the GazeFollow and GazeHOI benchmarks. Qualitatively, our agent provides detailed semantic predictions, predicts the correct targets even when ground truth labels are wrong, and remains flexible without vocabulary constraints.

</details>


### [69] [Branch-Centric Tokenization and Test-Time Augmentation for Skeleton Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06218)
*Zhengyuan Li,Chuanyu Pan,Yuanming Hu,Raymond Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对自动骨架生成中的两个关键问题——分支结构编码和测试时计算利用不足，提出了统一自回归框架下的两项改进：分支中心化tokenization和视图增强生成。在Articulation-XL2.0数据集上将CD-J2B误差相较于最先进的Auto-Connect基线降低了16.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动骨架生成方法难以将分支结构有效编码为token序列，且未能充分利用测试时的计算资源来提升预测质量。

Method: （1）引入分支中心化tokenization，将结构相关的元素置于相邻位置，直接在序列中编码连接关系，相比BFS式序列化产生更紧凑的序列；（2）引入视图增强生成，作为测试时增强技术，对输入网格施加轴向旋转，将所有预测映射回统一坐标系，基于网格覆盖率和多视图预测一致性选择最终骨架。

Result: 该方法在骨架预测精度上优于现有最先进方法，在Articulation-XL2.0数据集上相较最强直接可比基线Auto-Connect将CD-J2B误差降低了16.9%，在非结构化网格上的定性结果进一步证明了对多样化输入的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 分支中心化tokenization和视图增强生成为自动骨架生成提供了有效的序列编码方案和测试时计算利用策略，显著提升了预测精度和泛化性。

Abstract: Automatic skeleton generation involves predicting both joint positions and skeletal connectivity. However, existing approaches struggle to encode branch structures into token sequences and do not use test-time computation effectively. We study these choices within a unified autoregressive framework. First, we introduce branch-centric tokenization, a branch-aware representation that places structurally related elements next to each other and encodes connectivity directly in the sequence. Compared with standard BFS-style serialization, this representation yields more compact sequences. Second, we introduce view-augmented generation, a test-time augmentation procedure that applies axis-aligned rotations to the input mesh, maps all predictions back to a common frame, and selects the final skeleton based on mesh coverage and consistency among predictions from different views. Experiments show that our method achieves better skeleton prediction accuracy than state-of-the-art methods. In particular, our method reduces the CD-J2B error by 16.9% on the Articulation-XL2.0 dataset compared to the strongest directly comparable baseline, Auto-Connect. Qualitative results on in-the-wild meshes further demonstrate generalization across diverse inputs.

</details>


### [70] [Spatial Attention Supervision for Defect Localization: Exploiting Ground-Truth Masks as Training Signal in Diffusion-Augmented Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06232)
*Sajjad Rezvani Boroujeni,Muskan Saraf,Gnana Tulasi Makineni,Tom Bush,Hossein Abedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将工业检测数据集中原本仅用于评估的真实缺陷掩码（ground-truth masks）重新用作分类网络训练时的空间监督信号，通过激活对齐损失引导卷积特征图关注缺陷区域，并结合DDPM图像增强实现混合监督训练。在MVTec-AD瓶装数据集上对85个模型（4个CNN骨干及Swin-V2-T基线）的评估显示，注意力引导训练可使基于激活的定位Pixel-AUROC最高提升+18.7%，且分类精度无显著下降。但PatchCore无监督方法仍是更强定位器（Pixel-AUROC=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测数据集中标注的缺陷掩码通常仅用于评估而被闲置。作者希望重新利用这些丰富的空间监督信息，在分类网络训练中同时教会模型"预测什么"和"看哪里"，从而在不牺牲分类性能的前提下提升模型的可解释性与定位能力。

Method: 提出基于激活的注意力对齐损失函数，将卷积特征图导向缺陷标准掩码区域。采用混合监督训练框架，兼容无掩码样本（如DDPM扩散模型生成的图像）。通过2x2数据/训练因子设计（有无DDPM增强 × 有无掩码监督），在四个CNN骨干与Swin-V2-T Transformer上进行五种子实验的系统评估。

Result: 注意力引导训练使Pixel-AUROC显著提升：EfficientNetB0 +18.0%（p=0.005, d=2.6），ResNet50 +18.7%（p=0.008），在8个CNN设置中4个显著。EfficientNetB0存在数据×训练模式交互（p=0.002），体现超可加效应（+13.6% vs 个体效应和+1.6%）。空间表征较弱的架构获益最大，而ConvNeXt-T因深度卷积激活的空间无信息性而无效。PatchCore保持最强定位性能（0.983）。

Conclusion: 现有评估掩码可被实际用作训练信号，能够可测量且可复现地改善缺陷分类模型的注意力定位能力，为工业视觉分类网络的空间可解释性提供了一条简单有效的提升路径。

Abstract: Ground-truth defect masks in industrial inspection datasets are typically reserved for evaluation. This paper repurposes them as spatial supervision signals during training of classification networks, teaching a model not just what to predict but where to look. The method adds an activation-based attention alignment loss that steers convolutional feature maps toward defect regions, in a mixed-supervision formulation that also accommodates samples without masks, such as diffusion-generated images. Combined with DDPM augmentation, synthetic images contribute quantity while masks contribute spatial precision. We evaluate 85 models (four CNN backbones under a 2x2 data/training factorial over five seeds, plus a Swin-V2-T transformer baseline) on the MVTec-AD bottle benchmark, with localization measured on held-out defect images excluded from classifier gradient updates. Main findings: (1) attention-guided training improves activation-based localization (Pixel-AUROC) by +18.0% for EfficientNetB0 with augmentation (p=0.005, Cohen's d=2.6) and +18.7% for ResNet50 (p=0.008), significant in four of eight CNN settings (uncorrected for multiple comparisons) with no significant change in classification; (2) for EfficientNetB0 a data x training-mode interaction is significant (p=0.002), consistent with a super-additive effect (+13.6% combined vs +1.6% summed individual effects); (3) architectures with weaker spatial representations benefit most, whereas ConvNeXt-T shows no effect, apparently because its depthwise-convolution activations yield spatially uninformative channel-mean maps; (4) unsupervised PatchCore remains the strongest localizer (Pixel-AUROC=0.983), contextualizing the supervised gains. These results show that existing evaluation masks can act as practical training signals that measurably and reproducibly improve where defect classifiers attend.

</details>


### [71] [VDiff-Bench: A Challenging Benchmark for Fine-Grained Image Difference Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06245)
*Yixin Wan,Tianle Zheng,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VDiff-Bench，一个用于细粒度图像差异识别的多选基准测试，包含1,756个四选一问题，覆盖10种变化类别。通过对11个SOTA多模态大模型的评估，发现模型在低层变化（如噪声、纹理）上表现严重不足，揭示了标准单图视觉任务无法捕捉的对比视觉理解缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在通用视觉理解任务（如VQA）上表现优异，但在基本的图像比较技能——识别两幅相似图像之间的变化——上经常失败。现有的单图像视觉-语言任务无法覆盖这种对比式视觉理解能力，需要专门的诊断基准来暴露这些缺陷。

Method: 构建VDiff-Bench基准：包含1,756个四选一问题，每问题包含两张图像输入和4个选项（真实差异、两个基于真实标签构造的困难负样本、一个"无差异"干扰项）。覆盖10种变化类别：位置、运动、区域颜色、整体颜色、出现/消失、噪声/分辨率、纹理、替换/大小、OCR/文本、光照。实验对11个SOTA开源及闭源MLLM进行系统评估。

Result: 模型在细粒度视觉比较上表现不均衡：三个7-8B规模开源MLLM在语义变化上得分52.5-70.6%，但在噪声和纹理等低层变化上仅得分8.7-33.3%，常错误地假设两图无变化。此外，Grok 4.3在噪声和纹理识别上出现显著性能下降，落后于大型开源模型如Kimi K2.5和K3。

Conclusion: VDiff-Bench为评估MLLM的对比视觉理解提供了针对性诊断工具，揭示了标准单图视觉-语言任务无法捕获的系统性失败模式，尤其暴露了模型在低层视觉变化识别上的严重短板。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) perform strongly on general visual understanding tasks such as visual question answering, yet they often struggle with a basic comparative skill: identifying what has changed between two similar images. We introduce VDiff-Bench, a challenging multiple-choice benchmark for fine-grained Image Difference Identification. VDiff-Bench contains 1,756 four-way questions over image pairs and covers 10 change categories: position, motion, regional image color, overall image color, appearance/disappearance, noise/resolution, texture, substitution/size, OCR/text, and illumination. Each question corresponds to two image inputs with 4 choices: the true difference, two hard negative descriptions, and a "no difference" distractor. To make the task challenging, we specifically curate ground-truth-conditioned negatives that require models to distinguish the actual change from nearby semantic alternatives. Experiments with 11 state-of-the-art open- and closed-source MLLMs show that fine-grained visual comparison remains brittle: models exhibit uneven performance across sources and change categories, with persistent failures on subtle low-level changes like noises and textures. For instance, three 7-8B-scale open-source MLLMs score 52.5-70.6% on semantic changes but only 8.7-33.3% on low-level changes like noise and texture, falsely assuming no changes between two image inputs. Surprisingly, despite strong performance of other closed-source commercial models, Grok 4.3 demonstrate remarkable performance drop on identifying noise and texture differences between images, falling significantly behind large open-source models like Kimi K2.5 and K3. Overall, VDiff-Bench provides a targeted diagnostic for evaluating comparative visual understanding in MLLMs, exposing failures that are not captured by standard single-image vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [72] [MobileVLA-R1 2.0: RL-Enhanced Reasoning for Mobile Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06251)
*Ting Huang,Yue Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileVLA-R1 2.0，一个通过强化学习增强的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，显式地将结构化具身推理与可执行的移动机器人控制相结合，在导航、四足控制和类人移动操作等任务上优于现有VLA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人的VLA系统在高层次语义推理与低层次运动控制之间存在鸿沟，现有方法依赖隐式推理或单一动作预测，难以在长时程决策中保持连贯性，同时生成精确且可适应的机器人动作。

Method: 该框架通过监督式Chain-of-Thought(CoT)对齐和强化学习学习多粒度推理，并提出推理条件动作解码器，将多模态推理表示映射为任务级动作目标，再通过机器人控制器转化为具体指令，实现感知-推理-动作的统一接口并解耦高层动作生成与机器人特定执行。

Result: 在VLN-CE上平均提升1.6点SR，在真实G1移动操作任务上比MobileVLA-R1提升10.0点完整任务成功率，并在Unitree Go2和G1机器人上展现出鲁棒的长期指令跟随和闭环执行能力。

Conclusion: MobileVLA-R1 2.0通过显式推理-动作绑定和统一的感知-推理-动作接口，有效弥合了高层推理与低层控制在跨平台移动机器人场景中的差距，验证了该框架在不同机器人平台上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Grounding natural-language instructions into reliable and executable actions remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language-action (VLA) systems on mobile robots, due to the persistent gap between high-level semantic reasoning and low-level locomotion and manipulation control. Existing approaches often rely on implicit reasoning or monolithic action prediction, making it difficult to maintain coherent long-horizon decision making while producing precise and adaptable robot actions. To address this challenge, we propose MobileVLA-R1 2.0, an RL-enhanced VLA framework that explicitly couples structured embodied reasoning with executable mobile robot control. The framework learns multi-granularity reasoning over embodied trajectories through supervised Chain-of-Thought (CoT) alignment and reinforcement learning, improving reasoning-to-action consistency beyond purely behavioral supervision. To support both locomotion and manipulation, we further introduce a reasoning-conditioned action decoder that maps multimodal reasoning representations to task-level action targets, which are subsequently translated into embodiment-specific commands by robot controllers. This design provides a unified perception-reasoning-action interface while decoupling high-level action generation from robot-specific actuation. We conduct extensive evaluations on language-guided navigation, quadruped control, and humanoid mobile manipulation, covering VLN-CE, QUARD, and real-world deployments on Unitree Go2 and G1 robots. MobileVLA-R1 2.0 consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines, achieving an average 1.6 point improvement in SR on VLN-CE and a 10.0 point improvement in full-task success on real-world G1 mobile manipulation tasks over MobileVLA-R1, while demonstrating robust long-horizon instruction following and closed-loop execution across different robotic platforms.

</details>


### [73] [One Model, Two Worlds: Bidirectional Sonar-Optical Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06253)
*Shengji Jin,Trung Tien Dong,Ahmed Lamidi,Chen Chen,Xiaomin Lin,Yi Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DARB（方向非对称现实主义桥）与ARS（自适应现实主义监督），通过共享扩散桥主干、分方向路由物理先验以及自适应感知监督调度，实现单一双向模型同时支撑成像声纳与光学相机间的双向翻译，性能接近甚至超过独立训练的单向专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像声纳与光学相机之间的双向翻译对水下感知有价值，但用两个独立模型分别支撑两个方向会重复存储和计算；现有统一双向模型大多对称处理两个方向，忽略了二者在成像物理机制上的根本差异。

Method: 提出DARB保留共享扩散桥主干，通过非对称路径路由方向特定物理先验：声纳到光学采用距离感知调制，光学到声纳采用极线射线相关处理；同时提出ARS，根据重建质量和梯度平衡自适应决定感知监督的时机、位置和强度，打破对称训练带来的性能损失。

Result: DARB与ARS联合使单一双向模型在声纳到光学方向与专家模型仅差0.11 dB PSNR，在光学到声纳方向比专家模型FID提升0.70，并在八个指标中的七个上超越两个独立训练的BBDM模型。

Conclusion: 共享生成模型不必共享物理机制，方向非对称的设计能够更有效地统一双向成像翻译任务，同时减少模型存储和计算开销。

Abstract: Translating between imaging sonar and optical cameras is valuable for underwater perception, but supporting both directions with separate models duplicates storage and computation. A unified bidirectional model is therefore attractive, yet existing approaches largely treat the two directions symmetrically despite their fundamentally different image-formation physics. We argue that sharing a generative model does not require sharing the physics. We introduce the Direction-Asymmetric Realism Bridge (DARB), which retains a shared diffusion-bridge trunk while routing direction-specific physical priors through asymmetric pathways: range-aware modulation for sonar-to-optical translation and polar ray-dependent processing for optical-to-sonar translation. We further show that symmetry in training is also costly: applying a common realism schedule reduces sonar-to-optical PSNR by 2.60 dB. Our Adaptive Realism Supervision (ARS) instead determines when, where, and how strongly perceptual supervision is applied from reconstruction quality and gradient balance. Together, DARB and ARS enable one bidirectional model to match the sonar-to-optical specialist within 0.11 dB PSNR, outperform the optical-to-sonar specialist by 0.70 FID, and surpass two independently trained BBDMs on seven of eight metrics.

</details>


### [74] [Adapting Vision Foundation Models to Acoustics for Pose-Free 3D Sonar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06261)
*Kevin Zhang,Jingxi Chen,Mohamad Qadri,Russell Shomberg,Michael Kaess,Jia-Bin Huang,Adithya Pediredla,Christopher Metzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉基础模型高效适配到声呐领域的方法，通过利用两种传感模态的几何关系以及基于物理的精确噪声模型进行合成数据生成，首次实验性地实现了基于声呐的无位姿3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中浊度和低能见度条件使常规RGB基础模型无法应用，需要声呐基础模型；但缺乏大规模开源声呐数据集，从头训练不可行。

Method: （1）利用视觉与声呐两种传感模态之间的几何关系进行适配；（2）采用精确的基于物理的噪声模型生成合成声呐数据进行训练。

Result: 实验证明所提出的声呐适配模型首次实现了基于声呐的无位姿3D重建。

Conclusion: 视觉基础模型可以通过几何关系和物理噪声模型高效适配到声呐领域，从而在水下场景中实现类似视觉基础模型的新能力，弥补了缺乏大规模声呐数据集的不足。

Abstract: Vision foundation models trained on Internet-scale RGB datasets enable remarkable capabilities across a range of tasks, from text-to-video generation to few-shot 3D scene reconstruction. An acoustic foundation model trained on large-scale sonar datasets could enable similar capabilities in the underwater domain, where turbidity and low-visibility conditions make conventional RGB foundation models inapplicable. Unfortunately, a lack of freely available large-scale sonar datasets makes training such a model from scratch impractical. In this work, we demonstrate that vision foundation models can be efficiently adapted to the sonar setting by (1) exploiting the geometric relationship between the two sensing modalities and (2) employing accurate physics-based noise models for synthetic data generation. The resulting sonar adaptation models enable new capabilities: For the first time, we experimentally demonstrate sonar-based pose-free 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [75] [A Comparative Study of GAN-Based Deep Learning Models for Pneumonia Detection in Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06276)
*Roshan Paudel,Aashish Ghimire,Pramod Acharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了VGG19、MobileNetV2、ResNet50及自定义CNN在胸片肺炎分类中的表现，并探索GAN合成数据增强的效果。MobileNetV2准确率最高达88%；自定义CNN肺炎召回率92.67%但精确率仅79.43%。GAN增强实验显示训练准确率近100%但验证准确率仅约50%，未证明能提升验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺炎是严重的公共健康问题，胸部X光片是主要诊断工具之一，但人工阅片耗时且依赖经验。研究旨在评估多种深度学习模型在肺炎分类中的性能，并探究GAN合成数据增强是否能进一步提升分类效果。

Method: 使用四种模型（VGG19、MobileNetV2、ResNet50及自定义CNN）对胸片进行肺炎分类。采用GAN生成合成肺炎图像并与真实图像组合进行数据增强实验，使用准确率、F1分数、精确率、召回率、混淆矩阵及训练曲线等指标评估性能。

Result: MobileNetV2取得最高准确率88%，且类别间性能均衡。自定义CNN肺炎召回率92.67%，精确率79.43%，存在典型的精确率-召回率权衡。GAN增强实验中训练准确率接近100%，但验证准确率仅约50%，低于真实数据的验证准确率，未显示出验证性能提升。

Conclusion: 模型比较揭示了不同架构在准确率和肺炎召回率上的差异；GAN增强实验未能证明对验证集性能有提升，表明需进一步评估合成图像质量及训练设置。

Abstract: This study evaluates pneumonia classification in chest X-rays using VGG19, MobileNetV2, ResNet50, and a custom CNN, and explores Generative Adversarial Network (GAN)-based synthetic data augmentation. MobileNetV2 achieved the highest reported accuracy of 88% with balanced class-wise performance. The custom CNN achieved pneumonia recall of 92.67% and precision of 79.43%, highlighting a precision-recall trade-off. Accuracy, F1-score, precision, recall, confusion matrices, and training curves were used to assess performance.
  Synthetic pneumonia images were combined with real images to investigate whether augmentation could improve classification performance. In the reported VGG19 comparison, augmented-data training accuracy reached approximately 100%, while validation accuracy remained near 50%, below the real-data validation accuracy. This experiment therefore did not demonstrate a validation-performance benefit from GAN augmentation. The classifier comparison highlights differences in accuracy and pneumonia recall, while the augmentation experiment indicates the need for further evaluation of synthetic-image quality and training settings.

</details>


### [76] [Object-Aware Background-Controlled Editing via Weighted Velocity Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06288)
*Wuji Wang,Yue Wu,Chenhao Yi,Shuhui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OAVC（Object-Aware Velocity Control），一个无需训练的图像编辑框架，通过在速度积分过程中引入对象级控制来解耦语义残差的施加位置与注入方式，从而解决对象中心编辑中背景漂移和对象边界不稳定问题。实验表明OAVC在图像和视频基准上优于现有方法，提升背景保持、结构保真、边界稳定性和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于速度的免训练图像编辑器通常在潜在空间上全局施加提示残差，依赖模型隐式定位语义变化。对于对象中心编辑，目标对象之外的残差往往非零，在多步积分过程中微小非目标分量会累积，导致背景漂移和对象边界不稳定。

Method: OAVC将语义残差的作用位置与注入动力学的方式解耦。具体包括：1）在源提示下构建背景锚定参考界面；2）在目标提示下进行对象局部化的安全语义注入；3）通过约束注入算子抑制导致漂移的速度分量；4）采用时间自适应空间权重稳定对象边界附近的过渡。

Result: 在基于图像和视频整流流（rectified-flow）骨干网络的对象中心图像与视频基准上，OAVC展现出改进的背景保留、结构保真度、边界稳定性和时间一致性，同时保持有效的局部可编辑性。

Conclusion: OAVC作为一种无需训练、不改动预训练模型参数的框架，通过对象级速度控制有效解决了背景漂移和边界不稳定问题，为对象中心图像和视频编辑提供了一种稳健且高效的新方案。

Abstract: Training-free image editing steers diffusion or flow-matching generative models at inference time by modifying prompt-conditioned denoising velocities. Existing velocity-based editors often apply prompt-induced residuals globally over the latent space and rely on the model to localize semantic changes implicitly. For object-centric edits, these residuals are rarely zero outside the target object, so small non-target components can accumulate during multi-step integration, causing background drift and unstable object boundaries. We propose Object-Aware Velocity Control (OAVC), a training-free framework that introduces object-level control into the velocity-integration process. OAVC decouples where semantic residuals are allowed to act from how they are injected into the dynamics. It constructs a background-anchored reference interface under the source prompt and then performs object-localized safe semantic injection under the target prompt. A constrained injection operator suppresses drift-inducing velocity components, while time-adaptive spatial weighting stabilizes the transition near object boundaries. OAVC requires no training or modification of pretrained model parameters. Experiments on object-centric image and video benchmarks with image and video rectified-flow backbones show improved background preservation, structural fidelity, boundary stability, and temporal consistency while retaining effective localized editability.

</details>


### [77] [SignDino: Self-Supervised Sign Language Representation Learning via Temporal-Axis Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06296)
*Junyi Hu,Zhewen He,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Zhifei Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignDino是一个自监督手语视频编码器，将DINOv3的学生-教师框架从图像空间迁移到手语流的时间域，分解左右手和面部轨迹流，仅学习关节运动的时间演化而不改变图像级视觉特征，在翻译、识别和指拼检测任务上达到竞争性或SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语表征学习需要建模两个自然图像自监督学习不涉及的关键属性：手语由少量解剖学上不同的发音器官（面部和双手）产生，且语义取决于这些器官的时间组织结构。现有方法未能在自监督框架中同时建模这两个特性。

Method: 将每一个手语视频通过YOLOv8n+ByteTrack检测器分解为左手、右手和面部三种轨迹流。使用冻结的DINOv3 ViT-B/16对每帧解剖学裁剪区域进行嵌入，而轻量级时间Transformer作为学生和EMA教师。训练采用时间域DINO自蒸馏、iBOT风格帧级掩码标记预测、KoLeo特征扩展和Gram锚定的帧间相似性结构约束，仅学习关节状态的时间演化。

Result: 在手语到英语翻译、孤立手语识别和指拼检测三大基准任务上进行了评估。SignDino作为公开的自监督表征展现出强竞争力，在匹配的下游评估设置下达到竞争性或最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将DINOv3策略从空间域转向时间域，并固定图像级视觉基元、仅学习关节状态的时间演化，SignDino为手语理解提供了有效的自监督表征学习方法，证明了手语特有的架构设计优于直接迁移自然图像SSL方法。

Abstract: Self-supervised sign language representation learning must model two properties not central to natural-image SSL: signs are produced by a small set of anatomically distinct articulators, and their meaning depends on the temporal organisation of those articulators. We introduce SignDino, a self-supervised sign-video encoder that moves the DINOv3 student--teacher recipe from the spatial domain of image crops to the temporal domain of tracked sign streams. Each video is decomposed into left-hand, right-hand, and face streams by a detector-first YOLOv8n+ByteTrack pipeline. A frozen DINOv3 ViT-B/16 embeds each per-frame anatomical crop, while lightweight temporal Transformers, not the image backbone, form the student and EMA teacher. They are trained by temporal DINO self-distillation, frame-level masked-token prediction in the style of iBOT, KoLeo feature spreading, and Gram anchoring of the frame-to-frame similarity structure. This design keeps strong image-level visual primitives fixed and learns only how articulator states evolve across time. We evaluate on sign-to-English translation, isolated sign recognition, and fingerspelling detection benchmarks. Across these tasks, SignDino provides a strong public self-supervised representation and shows competitive or state-of-the-art performance under matched downstream evaluation.

</details>


### [78] [CST-WM: A Causally Structured World Model for Embodied Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06302)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CST-WM，一种因果结构化的世界模型，用于具身视觉跟踪任务。该模型将潜在状态分解为目标证据、机器人和观测三个分支，并通过阻断动作到目标证据分支的直接注入来消除"因果幻觉"（即模型利用动作与目标观测的相关性产生语义错误的未来预测），配合基于rollout的模型预测控制实现稳定的目标跟随与临时重捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉跟踪本质上是一个关于未来目标可观测性和表观尺度的预测性决策问题，但现有模型在动作条件预测中存在任务特有的因果幻觉：模型会利用机器人控制与目标观测之间的强相关性，幻觉出动作对目标证据的直接因果效应，而忽略了动作只能通过机器人运动和观测变化间接影响目标证据。这种捷径虽然产生貌似合理的未来，但语义错误，无法用于跟踪导向的规划和目标重捕获。

Method: CST-WM将潜在状态分解为目标证据、机器人和观测三个分支，对状态转移进行因果因子分解，阻断动作向目标证据分支的直接注入，同时保留动作对机器人运动及观测更新的作用。在该结构化的世界模型之上，结合基于rollout的模型预测控制框架，使稳定跟随和临时目标重捕获能在同一个规划框架内实现。

Result: 在EVT-Bench和Habitat 3.0基准上（覆盖标准跟踪、目标丢失恢复和跨数据集迁移），CST-WM在跟随质量、距离范围控制、安全性和重捕获能力上优于反应式基线和世界模型基线。离线诊断显示其具有更好的多步rollout保真度、更强的规划价值一致性，并显著减少了直接动作泄漏。

Conclusion: 对于具身视觉跟踪而言，仅做未来预测是不够的：预测结构本身必须与目标证据进入规划的因果路径对齐，即预测模型的结构性设计直接决定了其在跟踪规划和目标找回任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied visual tracking requires a robot not only to react to the current view, but to choose actions that preserve or recover future evidence of a moving target under ego-motion, occlusion, and distractors. It is therefore a predictive decision problem over future target observability and apparent scale. A central difficulty is a task-specific form of causal hallucination: in action-conditioned prediction, a model can exploit the strong correlation between robot control and target-related observations by hallucinating a direct causal effect from the current action to target evidence, rather than letting action influence that evidence only through robot motion and the resulting observation change. The shortcut yields plausible futures with the wrong semantics for tracking-oriented planning and re-acquisition. We propose CST-WM, a causally structured world model that decomposes the latent state into target-evidence, robot, and observation branches and factorizes the transition so that direct action injection into the target-evidence branch is blocked, while action remains available to robot motion and observation updates. Combined with rollout-based model-predictive control, CST-WM supports both stable following and temporary target re-acquisition in one planning framework. On EVT-Bench and Habitat 3.0, covering standard tracking, target-loss recovery, and cross-dataset transfer, it improves following quality, distance-range control, safety, and re-acquisition over reactive and world-model baselines; offline diagnostics show better multi-step rollout fidelity, stronger planning-value consistency, and substantially reduced direct action leakage. For embodied visual tracking, future prediction alone is not enough: the predictive structure itself must align with how target evidence enters planning.

</details>


### [79] [One Shared LoRA Weight for MRI Reconstruction across Acceleration Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06338)
*Zhiwei Zhao,Weikang Gong,Zhongnian Li,Xinzheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Shared LoRA框架，通过冻结预训练的SHFormer骨干网络并仅训练一组共享LoRA适配器和轻量级门控网络，实现跨加速因子的参数高效MRI重建，可训练参数仅占约5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI重建方法针对不同加速因子需分别训练独立模型，导致跨因子泛化能力差且训练和存储成本高昂。

Method: 冻结预训练SHFormer骨干，训练单一共享LoRA适配器集与轻量级门控网络（GateNet）。训练时随机采样加速因子及其采样掩码生成欠采样输入，GateNet根据加速因子生成逐层系数以动态调制各适配器的残差强度。

Result: 在多个加速因子上达到最优或相当的PSNR和SSIM性能，可训练参数仅占总模型参数的约5.3%；低加速因子性能不会因联合训练因子集扩大而明显下降，并能稳定泛化到未见过的邻近因子。

Conclusion: Shared LoRA以参数高效方式实现了跨加速因子的统一MRI重建，兼顾了性能、泛化能力和训练/存储效率。

Abstract: Accelerated MRI reconstruction recovers images from undersampled k-space. However, different acceleration factors produce distinct artifact patterns. Existing methods often train separate models for each factor, leading to poor cross-factor generalization and high training and storage costs. We propose Shared LoRA, a parameter-efficient framework that freezes the pretrained SHFormer backbone and trains a single shared set of LoRA adapters together with a lightweight gating network. During training, undersampled inputs are generated by randomly sampling acceleration factors and their corresponding sampling masks, enabling the shared adapters to learn reconstruction knowledge across factors. Given the acceleration factor, GateNet generates layer-wise coefficients to dynamically modulate the residual strength of each adapter. Experiments show that Shared LoRA achieves the best or competitive PSNR and SSIM across acceleration factors, while its trainable parameters account for only about 5.3% of the total model parameters. Its performance at lower acceleration factors remains largely unaffected as the jointly trained factor set expands, and it generalizes stably to unseen neighboring factors.

</details>


### [80] [Radiation, Rotation and Scale Invariant Feature Descriptor for Multimodal Image Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06343)
*Yuanxin Ye,Tengfeng Tang,Tao Peng,Zhiqiang Han,Jiayuan Li,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种辐射、旋转和尺度不变（RRSI）的多模态图像特征描述子，通过双头区域采样（DHRS）模块联合编码几何与辐射关系，并引入双向跨模态生成重建约束来锚定模态不变的几何拓扑，在光学-红外和光学-SAR等数据集上展现了出色的匹配性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像匹配是多源信息融合的基础任务，但几何畸变和非线性辐射差异（NRD）严重限制了匹配性能，尤其是在辐射、旋转和尺度变化下表现不佳。现有方法难以同时应对这些挑战，亟需一种对辐射、旋转和尺度均具有鲁棒性的特征描述方法。

Method: 首先提出双头区域采样（DHRS）模块，在关键点邻域同时执行笛卡尔和对数极坐标采样，保留空间结构特性的同时增强对旋转和尺度变化的鲁棒性。然后，在统一深度特征空间中联合编码几何与辐射关系，实现模态内、双头采样和模态间区域的特征编码、交互与融合。此外，引入双向跨模态生成重建约束，通过将隐式特征解码为对应模态的结构块来锚定模态不变的几何拓扑，且不增加推理开销。

Result: 在光学-红外和光学-SAR数据集上的实验表明，该方法具有高度竞争力的匹配性能和强大的旋转/尺度鲁棒性，支持0至360度全范围旋转及最高4倍的尺度因子。在计算机视觉、遥感和医学影像的多模态图像上进一步验证了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: RRSI特征描述子通过联合编码几何与辐射信息并引入跨模态生成重建约束，有效解决了多模态图像匹配中辐射、旋转和尺度变化的挑战，展现出良好的泛化能力，方法代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Multimodal image matching is a fundamental task for multi-source information fusion. However, geometric distortions and nonlinear radiometric differences (NRD) severely limit performance, especially under radiometric, rotation, and scale variations. To address this issue, we propose a radiation, rotation, and scale invariant (RRSI) feature descriptor. First, a dual-head regional sampling (DHRS) module simultaneously performs Cartesian and Log-Polar sampling on keypoint neighborhoods, retaining spatial structural properties while enhancing robustness to rotation and scale variations. We then jointly encode geometric and radiometric relations between multimodal images in a unified deep feature space, enabling feature encoding, interaction, and fusion across intra-modal, dual-head sampled, and inter-modal regions. Furthermore, we introduce a bidirectional cross-modal generative reconstruction constraint during training. By decoding implicit features into structural patches of the counterpart modality, this mechanism anchors modality-invariant geometric topologies without additional inference overhead. Experiments on optical-infrared and optical-SAR datasets demonstrate highly competitive matching performance and strong robustness to rotation and scale variations. RRSI supports the full rotation range from 0 to 360 degrees and scale factors up to four. Its generalization ability is further validated on multimodal images from computer vision, remote sensing, and medical imaging. The implementation will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yeyuanxin110/RRSI .

</details>


### [81] [ChildGaze: A Benchmark Dataset for Collaborative Behavior Understanding in Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06353)
*Sindhuja Penchala,Saketh Reddy Kontham,Prachi Bhattacharjee,S. Nima Mahmoodi,Daniel Fonseca,Sareh Karami,Mehdi Garemani,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChildGaze，一个基于ChildPlay视频集构建的儿童中心行为标注数据集，引入协作与非协作两种行为标签，独立标注每帧中每个儿童。数据集包含27个视频、10,641帧及73,268条身体部位标注行，标注可靠性高（Cohen's kappa 0.8631），基线实验使用ViT和Swin Transformer模型分别达到97.44%和96.80%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解儿童的协作行为对分析社会参与、同伴互动、共享注意力和参与度至关重要。然而，仅估计儿童的注视方向并不能揭示其是否积极参与共享活动，现有数据缺乏更高层次的行为标注支持这一分析。

Method: 构建ChildGaze数据集，为每帧中每个儿童独立标注协作/非协作行为标签，提供儿童和成人的脸、左右手边界框，并在行、人物和帧三个层级组织标注；使用预训练的ViT和Swin Transformer模型进行基线实验。

Result: 协作标签原始一致性达93.16%，Cohen's kappa为0.8631；边界框标注整体平均IoU为0.808。基线实验中，预训练ViT模型达到97.44%的儿童人物级准确率，Swin Transformer达到96.80%的帧级准确率。

Conclusion: ChildGaze为研究自然情境下儿童-成人及同伴互动中的协作行为提供了可靠的基准数据集，支持儿童发展研究、教育分析和以人为中心的计算机视觉研究。

Abstract: Understanding collaborative behavior in children is important for analyzing social participation, peer interaction, shared attention, and engagement during play and learning activities. Reliable recognition of these cues can support research in child development, educational analysis, and human-centered computer vision. However, estimating where a child is looking does not necessarily reveal whether the child is actively participating in a shared activity. To support this higher-level analysis, we introduce ChildGaze, a child-centered behavioral annotation dataset built on the ChildPlay video collection [1]. ChildGaze introduces two behavioral labels, collaborative and non-collaborative, assigned independently to each child within a frame. The dataset provides face, left-hand, and right-hand bounding boxes for children and adults and organizes the annotations at the row, person, and frame levels. The current release contains 27 annotated video files, 10,641 frames, and 73,268 body-part annotation rows. Annotation reliability was evaluated on 1,187 frames using independent annotations from two annotators. The collaboration labels achieved 93.16% raw agreement and a Cohen's kappa of 0.8631, while bounding-box annotations achieved an overall mean IoU of 0.808. Baseline experiments with pretrained ViT and Swin Transformer models achieved up to 97.44% child-person-level accuracy and 96.80% frame-level accuracy, respectively. These results show that ChildGaze provides a reliable benchmark for studying collaborative behavior in naturalistic child-adult and peer interactions.

</details>


### [82] [MSCA-UNet: Multi-Scale Context and Attention U-Net for Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06356)
*Sheng-Wei Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSCA-UNet，在U-Net瓶颈处引入多尺度空洞卷积捕获上下文特征，并在解码器中用CBAM进行通道-空间注意力细化，最终mIoU从基线96.9%提升至99.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U-Net虽然结构简单实用，但其瓶颈表示受限于有限的感受野，且解码器特征传播缺乏对最具信息量通道和空间位置的显式强调，限制了分割性能的进一步提升。

Method: MSCA-UNet在U-Net瓶颈处使用并行空洞卷积模块进行多尺度上下文聚合，同时在解码器中逐步引入卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）对特征进行通道和空间维度上的重新校准。

Result: 在相同实验条件下，基线U-Net为96.9% mIoU；仅加入多尺度上下文提升至97.5%；仅使用注意力达到98.4%；两者结合达到99.1% mIoU，较基线提升2.2个百分点。参数分析显示注意力变体仅增加约0.044M参数，而多尺度模块贡献了大部分额外模型容量。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了多尺度上下文增强与注意力特征细化在U-Net框架中具有互补性，两者结合可有效提升图像分割性能。

Abstract: U-Net remains a practical baseline for image segmentation because of its simple encoder-decoder structure and skip connections. However, the bottleneck representation is still dominated by a limited set of receptive fields, while decoder features are propagated without explicitly emphasizing the most informative channels and spatial locations. This paper presents MSCA-UNet, a U-Net-based segmentation architecture that combines multi-scale contextual aggregation at the bottleneck with channel-spatial attention refinement in the decoder. The multi-scale module uses parallel atrous convolutions to capture contextual features at different receptive fields, while Convolutional Block Attention Modules (CBAMs) progressively recalibrate decoder features. Under identical experimental settings, the baseline U-Net achieves 96.9% mIoU on a held-out test set. Adding multi-scale context improves mIoU to 97.5%, while attention alone reaches 98.4%. Combining both mechanisms yields 99.1% mIoU, a 2.2 percentage-point improvement over the baseline. Parameter analysis further shows that the attention-only variant adds approximately 0.044M parameters, whereas the multi-scale module contributes most of the additional model capacity. The results support the view that multi-scale context enrichment and attention-based feature refinement provide complementary benefits within a U-Net framework.

</details>


### [83] [AGSA-Net: Abundance-Guided Self-Attention Network for Spectral Unmixing-Aware Hyperspectral Remote Sensing Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06359)
*Nafisa Anjum,Satavisa Dey Borno,Ananna Saha,Mir Faiyaz Hossain,Sifat Momen,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGSA-Net，一种丰度引导的自注意力网络，将光谱解混先验显式集成到高光谱图像分类中，通过估计亚像素丰度图并构建丰度亲和力先验来引导光谱Transformer，以解决光谱冗余、噪声敏感及局部组成与长距离光谱依赖联合建模的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类在农业、环境监测和城市分析中至关重要，但面临三大挑战：高光谱冗余、噪声敏感、以及难以联合建模局部物质组成与长距离光谱依赖。现有方法仅将丰度作为辅助或拼接特征，未充分利用其物理意义来指导分类过程。

Method: AGSA-Net首先估计满足非负和和为1约束的亚像素丰度图，并用混合线性和非线性重建解码器进行正则化；然后利用学习到的丰度构建丰度亲和力先验，引导光谱Transformer聚焦于类别判别性交互；最后将Transformer特征与紧凑丰度描述符融合用于最终分类预测。

Result: 在Indian Pines、Augsburg和Berlin三个数据集上的实验验证了丰度引导上下文建模的有效性，特别是在异构城市场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 将光谱解混先验显式融入Transformer架构的丰度引导方案能够有效提升HSI分类性能，代码和模型已公开在GitHub上。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification plays a vital role in remote sensing applications, including agriculture, environmental monitoring, and urban analysis. However, its performance remains challenged by high spectral redundancy, noise sensitivity, and the difficulty of jointly modeling local material composition and long-range spectral dependencies. To address this, we propose AGSA-Net, an abundance-guided self-attention network that explicitly integrates spectral unmixing priors into the classification process. AGSA Net first estimates physically meaningful subpixel abundance maps subject to non-negativity and sum-to-one constraints, regularized by hybrid linear-nonlinear reconstruction decoder. The learned abundances are then used to construct an abundance affinity prior that guides a spectral transformer to emphasize class-discriminative interactions, and the resulting transformer features are fused with compact abundance descriptors for final prediction; in contrast to existing approaches that use abundance as auxiliary or concatenated features. Experiments on Indian Pines, Augsburg, and Berlin demonstrate the benefit of incorporating abundance- guided contextual modeling, particularly in heterogeneous urban scenes. The source code and trained models are available at: https://github.com/nnuvi/AGSA-Net

</details>


### [84] [NOVA: Normal-Side Modeling for Training-Free Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06360)
*Wei-Chih Yin,Yun-Ching Kao,Cheng-Kuan Lin,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA是一个无需训练的零样本视频异常检测框架，通过增强正常侧的语义和视觉表示来提升正常与异常的区分度，在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based的零样本视频异常检测方法过度关注异常侧语义，而对正常侧的建模不够精细。存在两个关键问题：(i)正常提示词中包含与异常语义相近的模糊动词（如running），导致正常与异常的决策边界模糊；(ii)文本正常锚点与视觉帧之间的模态差距导致特征对齐不佳。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Normality-Aware Prompt Construction (NA)：在语言层面排除与异常相邻的动词，将正常描述偏向静态、低运动场景；2) Visual Normality Anchor (VNA)：在视觉层面利用每个测试视频的初始帧构建加权的视觉正常锚点，提供视频特定的正常参考，无需任务特定训练或标注。

Result: 在UCF-Crime数据集上达到89.86% AUC，在XD-Violence数据集上达到95.07% AUC和84.82% AP，在可比较的无需训练零样本方法中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过同时强化正常侧的语言层面和视觉层面表示，NOVA有效解决了零样本视频异常检测中决策边界模糊和模态差距两大挑战，验证了在无需训练条件下提升正常侧建模质量的有效性。

Abstract: Training-free zero-shot video anomaly detection (ZS-VAD) leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to localize anomaly instances from a predefined anomaly vocabulary, without providing any video. Existing CLIP-based methods often emphasize anomaly-side semantics, while the competing normality side remains less carefully formulated. We identify two key limitations in existing solutions: (i) blurred decision boundary: normal prompts may contain ambiguous verbs, such as running, that are semantically close to anomalies, reducing normal and abnormal separation in the VLM embedding space; and (ii) modality gap: poor alignment between features of textual normal anchors and visual frames. We propose NOVA, a training-free ZS-VAD framework that strengthens the normal side at both linguistic and visual levels. NOVA introduces Normality-Aware Prompt Construction (NA), which excludes anomaly-adjacent verbs and biases normal descriptions toward static, low-motion scenes. To overcome the text-vision modality gap, NOVA constructs a Visual Normality Anchor (VNA), which creates a weighted visual normal anchor from the initial frames of each test video, providing a video-specific normal reference without task-specific training or annotations. NOVA achieves 89.86 percent AUC on UCF-Crime and 95.07 percent AUC and 84.82 percent AP on XD-Violence, reaching state-of-the-art performance among comparable training-free zero-shot methods.

</details>


### [85] [DualPathOcc: Dual-Resolution BEV Encoder for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06370)
*Lihao Qiu,Jian Chen,Ruihao Wang,Ramu Gautam,Mei Yang,Yingtao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualPathOcc是一个基于相机的3D占用预测框架，通过空间增强器、SENet增强的双路径BEV编码器和高度感知加权交叉熵损失，在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到37.37 mIoU，无需显式深度监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角图像的3D占用预测需要在2D到3D提升过程中保持几何细节，同时在稀疏的体积场景表示上进行推理，现有方法在这两方面存在不足。

Method: 提出DualPathOcc框架：1）空间增强器在BEV压缩前进行高分辨率特征聚合；2）SENet增强的双路径BEV编码器实现局部-全局上下文建模；3）高度感知加权交叉熵损失优化近地面占用预测；模型仅使用占用监督训练，不依赖显式深度损失。

Result: 在单帧Occ3D-nuScenes基准上达到37.37 mIoU。

Conclusion: DualPathOcc通过多组件协同设计有效提升了3D占用预测精度；此外，论文分析了以表面为中心（surface-centered）的深度目标如何与体积占用学习相互作用。

Abstract: Predicting 3D occupancy from multi-view images requires preserving geometric detail during 2D-to-3D lifting while reasoning over sparse, volumetric scene representations. We present DualPathOcc, a camera-based framework that combines a Spatial Enhancer for high-resolution feature aggregation before BEV compression, a SENet-augmented dual-path BEV encoder for local-global context modeling, and height-aware weighted cross-entropy for near-ground occupancy. The final model is optimized with occupancy supervision and no explicit depth loss. On single-frame Occ3D-nuScenes, DualPathOcc achieves 37.37 mIoU. We further analyze how surface-centered depth targets interact with volumetric occupancy learning.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-Grid Post-Training for Long-Form Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06373)
*Jiawei Mao,Haoqin Tu,Hardy Chen,Yuhan Wang,Keyang Xu,Jieru Mei,Hongliang Fei,Ruogu Fang,Wei Shao,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MovieGrid提出了一种多网格后训练范式，将长视频分解为短时段并排列在空间网格上进行联合建模，在相同token预算下生成比Temporal Packing多6.05倍的镜头，并在镜头内和镜头间一致性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成器擅长连续运动，但当整个叙事压缩在单一时间轴时难以呈现完整的镜头集合，无法同时保证镜头内的连贯运动和跨镜头的视觉叙事一致性。

Method: 提出MovieGrid多网格后训练范式：将长视频分解为时间有序的短块并排列在空间网格上联合建模；构建MGLV数据集（从1000个长视频生成54K网格视频）；采用无噪声随机网格训练保留干净上下文进行去噪；网格嵌入编码结构、角色感知故事提示关联重复实体、网格边界损失稳定布局。

Result: 在相同token预算下，MovieGrid生成1,616帧视频的镜头数比Temporal Packing多6.05倍；在五个真实世界类别的基准上，镜头内一致性达0.9131（优于HoloCine的0.8086），镜头间一致性达0.5914（优于StoryMem的0.5384）；可通过单次或多次生成进一步扩展视频长度。

Conclusion: MovieGrid通过空间网格化的多镜头联合建模，有效解决了长视频生成中镜头完整呈现与叙事一致性问题，并能以最小的性能折损扩展视频长度，为长格式多镜头视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generating long-form multi-shot videos requires coherent within-shot motion and visually consistent narratives across shots. Existing video generators favor continuous motion and struggle to present complete shot sets when an entire narrative is packed along one temporal axis. We propose MovieGrid, a Multi-Grid Post-Training paradigm that decomposes a long video into shorter, temporally ordered chunks and arranges them on a spatial grid for joint modeling. This design reduces the number of shots handled by each temporal axis while enabling global information exchange across chunks. We construct the Multi-Grid Long Video (MGLV) dataset from 1,000 long-form videos using source video collection, hierarchical segmentation, grid video construction, and character-aware story annotation, producing 54K grid videos paired with story prompts. Our Noise-Free Random-Grid Training retains a random subset of chunks as clean visual context for denoising the remaining chunks. Grid Embedding encodes grid structure, character-aware Story Prompts link recurring entities, and Grid Boundary Loss stabilizes layouts. Under the same token budget, MovieGrid generates 6.05 times more shots than Temporal Packing in a 1,616-frame video. On a benchmark spanning five real-world categories, it achieves state-of-the-art intra-shot consistency (0.9131 versus 0.8086 for HoloCine) and inter-shot consistency (0.5914 versus 0.5384 for StoryMem). MovieGrid can further scale video length with minimal compromise through single or multiple generations.

</details>


### [87] [An Integrated Video-AI Platform for Action-Level Microanastomosis Training and Performance Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06380)
*Yan Meng,Daniel A. Donoho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成视频-AI平台，结合动作分割transformer、物体检测追踪和grounded大语言模型，将显微血管吻合手术视频转化为可检查的交互式反馈，支持规模化形成性微外科训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微吻合技能训练需要及时的、针对动作的反馈，但专家审阅长时间显微视频无法扩展到频繁或分布式训练场景，缺乏可扩展的自动化反馈机制。

Method: 三个模块：1）transformer将视频分割为六个手术动作；2）物体检测与追踪定位器械尖端，提取运动学特征和动作统计数据，驱动五个NOMAT对齐绩效维度的监督分类；3）grounded大语言模型利用结构化输出回答用户关于场景、动作、运动和预测绩效的问题。

Result: 两中心研究中，17名参与者完成72个手术流程共576次缝合。动作分割准确率87.66%、F1 82.86%，流程感知精炼后提升至93.62%和88.32%。五个绩效分类器平均准确率76.0%，Cohen's κ为0.63-0.93。

Conclusion: 该平台建立了专家监督训练系统的技术基础，可缩短审阅时间、揭示绩效估计背后的证据、支持可扩展的形成性显微外科训练。语言界面和教育效益仍需前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Developing microanastomosis skill requires repeated practice with timely, action-specific feedback, yet expert review of lengthy microscope videos does not scale to frequent or distributed training. We present an integrated video-AI platform that turns a complete simulated procedure into inspectable, interactive feedback through three connected modules. First, a proposed transformer segments the video into six surgical actions. Second, object detection and tracking localize instrument tips within each action; the resulting kinematic features and action statistics drive supervised classification of five NOMAT-aligned performance dimensions. Third, a grounded large language model (LLM) uses these structured outputs to answer user questions about the current scene, actions, motion, and predicted performance through a unified interface. In a two-site study, 17 participants completed 72 procedures comprising 576 suture placements. The action-segmentation module achieved 87.66\% accuracy and 82.86\% F1, increasing to 93.62\% and 88.32\% after workflow-aware refinement. The five performance classifiers achieved 76.0\% mean accuracy, with Cohen's $κ$ from 0.63 to 0.93. Although the language interface and educational benefit require prospective evaluation, these results establish the technical basis for an expert-supervised platform that can shorten review, expose the evidence behind performance estimates, and support scalable formative microsurgical training.

</details>


### [88] [Scaling 3D Generative Priors to Large-Scale Scene Meshes from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06385)
*SangEun Lee,Wonseok Chae,Hoyoung Yoo,Geunyong Kim,NackWoo Kim,Hyeonjin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的大规模纹理网格生成框架，通过局部上下文分块生成和全局外观对齐，解决多视图大场景生成中几何连续性和外观一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练3D生成模型主要用于物体级生成，空间范围有限。现有方法通过划分场景为多个区域来扩展，但扩展到大规模多视图场景时难以保持局部几何连续性和全局外观一致性。

Method: 1) 局部上下文分块生成：改善相邻区域间的几何连续性；2) 全局外观对齐：减少远距离区域间的外观差异；3) 自适应场景分解：根据输入场景几何形状确定分块数量。

Result: 实验表明该方法在几何和外观保真度上优于现有方法，同时支持大规模场景的细粒度生成。

Conclusion: 通过训练免费的方式实现局部与全局的协调生成，有效提升了大规模场景生成的几何连续性和外观一致性。

Abstract: Pretrained 3D generative models produce detailed geometry and appearance but are primarily designed for object-centric generation within a limited spatial extent. Recent approaches address this limitation by partitioning large scenes into smaller spatial regions and applying pretrained 3D generative priors to each region. However, scaling tiled generation to large multi-view scenes makes it challenging to maintain local geometric continuity and global appearance consistency.
  We present a training-free framework for large-scale textured mesh generation from multi-view images. Our key idea is to scale tiled generation to large scenes with increased spatial detail while coordinating generation both locally and globally. We introduce local context tiled generation to improve geometric continuity between neighboring regions and global appearance alignment to reduce appearance discrepancies across distant regions. An adaptive scene decomposition further determines the number of tiles according to the input scene geometry. Experiments demonstrate improved geometric and appearance fidelity over existing approaches while enabling fine-grained generation of large-scale scenes.

</details>


### [89] [Separating Capability from Confidence: Grounded Dual-State Calibration for GRPO-Trained Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06419)
*Yangyang Xie,Ke Hao,Jiaqi Liu,Yun Gu,Xinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DualRead框架，将能力学习与置信度估计分离，并通过CCG-AUC指标评估置信度是否反映视觉依据，从而提升医学视觉语言模型的置信度可靠性与校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的方法将口头置信度与答案生成联合优化，可能导致两者相互干扰并使置信度趋于二值化，且口头置信度无法显式评估视觉支持程度，缺乏反映患者特定视觉证据的可信置信度。

Method: 将能力学习与置信度估计解耦。冻结GRPO训练的actor，结合答题前可解性判断与答题后对生成答案及其视觉支持的评估。同时提出CCG-AUC指标，通过真实图像替换导致actor从正确变错误时置信度是否下降来评估置信度是否反映视觉依据。

Result: 在两个VLM骨干网络上及分布内和分布外的医学VQA基准中，DualRead在保持答案准确性的同时，相比口头置信度方法提升了正确性判别能力和校准效果。CCG-AUC揭示了置信度是否对与答案相关的视觉证据做出响应，而非主要依赖非视觉线索。

Conclusion: 分离能力学习与置信度估计是提升医学VLM置信度可靠性的有效途径，CCG-AUC为评估置信度与视觉依据的关联提供了新的度量标准，有助于推动医学VLMs在实际应用中更可信地部署。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) require confidence that reflects both answer correctness and patient-specific visual evidence. Recent GRPO-based methods optimize verbalized confidence together with answer generation. However, this joint optimization may interfere with answer learning and drive confidence toward near-binary values. Verbalized confidence also provides no explicit assessment of visual support. We therefore separate capability learning from confidence estimation and propose \textbf{DualRead}. DualRead builds on the insight that reliability can be read from the actor's internal states at critical moments in the answering process. It freezes the GRPO-trained actor and combines pre-answer solvability with a post-answer assessment of the generated answer and its visual support. To further assess whether confidence reflects visual grounding, we introduce \textbf{Counterfactual Confidence Grounding AUC} (CCG-AUC). It measures whether confidence decreases when real-image substitution changes the actor from correct to incorrect. Across two VLM backbones and both in- and out-of-distribution medical VQA benchmarks, DualRead improves correctness discrimination and calibration over verbalized confidence while preserving answer accuracy. CCG-AUC reveals whether confidence responds to answer-relevant visual evidence rather than primarily to non-visual cues.

</details>


### [90] [PLSR: Progressive and Localized Super-Resolution of 3D Objects via Localized Latent Voxel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06436)
*Yuxin Liu,Minshan Xie,Jiawen Liang,Runsong Zhu,Chi-Wing Fu,Tien-Tsin Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PLSR框架，通过渐进式和局部化的超分辨率方法，在现有3D生成模型基础上高效生成高分辨率、富含细节的3D物体，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的3D生成方法受限于固定分辨率，难以生成高细节的3D物体，因此需要一种能在高分辨率下有效生成细节的解决方案。

Method: PLSR采用三个关键技术：1）通过关联输入分解方案将全局超分任务分解为局部子任务；2）通过低成本微调将基于流的3D生成器适配为局部超分辨率模型；3）将这些子任务统一到迭代的逐块去噪流程中，以生成无缝的高分辨率输出。

Result: 在具有挑战性的物体实验上，该方法能够生成具有强细节保真度的3D细节，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: PLSR为高分辨率3D资产生成提供了一种实用且高效的新解决方案，在不牺牲质量的前提下大幅提升细节生成能力。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D asset generation is vital in various 3D applications. Existing state-of-the-art diffusion-based models remain constrained by fixed resolutions, limiting their ability to produce details. In this paper, we tackle the challenge of generating more detailed, higher-resolution 3D objects by introducing a 3D super-resolution (SR) framework built on existing 3D generative foundation models. To this end, we design PLSR, a progressive and localized super-resolution solution to achieve this goal effectively and memory efficiently. Technically, given a coarse geometry from a pretrained 3D generator, we decompose the global SR task into localized sub-tasks via an associative input decomposition scheme, adapt a flow-based 3D generator into a localized super-resolution model through low-cost finetuning, and unify them in an iterative patch-wise denoising pipeline for seamless high-resolution output. Experiments on challenging objects show that our approach is able to generate 3D details with new strong fine-detail fidelity while significantly reducing the computational cost, offering a new and practical solution for high-resolution 3D asset generation.

</details>


### [91] [Large-Scale Pretraining for Improving Deep Learning-Based Geometric Distortion Correction of Diffusion-Weighted Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06437)
*Saroj Khanal,Yashawant Kumar Yadav,Kritam Bhattarai,Jeevan Neupane,Shristi Subedi,Saship Gwachha,Manish Kumar Tiwari,Dong Zhang,Confidence Raymond,Aondona Moses Iorumbur,Udunna Anazodo,Surendra Maharjan,Bishesh Khanal,Mahesh Shakya,Pralhad Kumar Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨利用大规模预训练策略改进基于深度学习的单相位编码扩散加权成像（DWI）几何畸变校正。实验比较了无预训练基线、自监督预训练和生成式预训练模型，结果显示生成式预训练模型（cWDM）在定量和定性评估中表现最佳；但在低中收入国家（LMIC）数据迁移时存在挑战，如对比度改变和过度依赖T1加权解剖结构，而图像配准至标准空间可改善跨域部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWI在临床中广泛使用但易受几何畸变影响，现有校正方法常需额外采集或厂商专属解决方案，在高通量、资源受限环境中难以推广，亟需探索更普适且高效的校正方法。

Method: 将畸变校正建模为图像重建任务，比较三种模型：无预训练基线、自监督预训练模型和生成式预训练模型（cWDM），采用定量图像相似性指标和定性专家评估进行评价，并对最优模型在LMIC环境采集移位数据上进行迁移测试，同时评估将图像配准到标准空间对预测的影响。

Result: 预训练模型整体优于无预训练基线，cWDM在定量与定性评估中均取得最佳效果。然而在LMIC数据上存在迁移挑战，包括对比度改变和过度依赖T1加权结构；将图像配准到共同标准空间可改善预测效果。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练能有效提升DWI畸变校正性能，但跨域部署仍需考虑数据域差异，通过标准空间配准等协调预处理手段可增强模型的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion-weighted imaging (DWI) is widely used in clinical settings but remains vulnerable to geometric distortion. Conventional correction methods often require additional acquisitions or vendor-specific solutions, limiting their feasibility in high-throughput, resource-constrained settings. This study investigates whether large-scale pretraining strategies can improve deep learning-based distortion correction for single-phase-encoding DWI. We formulate the task as image reconstruction, and compare a non-pretrained baseline against a self-supervised and a generative pretrained model, evaluated using both quantitative image-similarity metrics and qualitative expert assessment. The best-performing model was further tested for transferability on data collected in an LMIC setting with acquisition shift. Pretrained models outperformed the non-pretrained baseline, with cWDM achieving the strongest results across both quantitative and qualitative evaluation. However, application to LMIC data revealed transferability challenges, including contrast alteration and over-reliance on T1-weighted anatomical structure. Registering images to a common standard space improved predictions, suggesting that harmonized preprocessing may enhance cross-domain deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Thinking with Cameras: Active Visual Reasoning via Dynamic Viewpoint Control for Surveillance Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06475)
*Xiao Zhang,Wang Zeng,Sheng Jin,Wentao Liu,Chen Qian,Shichao Kan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CamVLM 是一个新颖的"用摄像头思考"框架，通过动态视角控制让大视觉-语言模型主动获取视觉证据，而非被动分析固定视频流。论文构建了两个大规模数据集（CCTV-Anomaly 包含14,459个异常视频，CamTrack-53K 用于学习摄像机动作），并提出了基于强化学习的视角策略优化方法，最终在被动观察和动态视角条件下均取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉-语言模型在通用视频理解上进展显著，但应用于监控视频时面临两大挑战：缺乏大规模领域特定数据集，以及固定视角下的被动观察导致关键视觉证据容易遗漏（目标过远、过小、被遮挡或移出视野）。现有方法无法主动获取视觉信息。

Method: 论文提出三部分内容：1) CCTV-Anomaly数据集，包含14,459个视频、10类异常事件及详细标注；2) CamTrack-53K数据集，将视角控制视为主动视觉感知问题，用于学习摄像机动作；3) 基于强化学习的视角策略优化框架，将摄像机控制建模为序列决策过程，学习超越监督式轨迹模仿的长时程观察策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，CamVLM在被动观察和动态视角设置下均达到最先进性能，验证了主动基于摄像头的推理对监控视频理解的有效性。

Conclusion: CamVLM首次将监控视频中的摄像机视角控制与LVLM结合，通过主动感知范式弥补被动观察的固有局限，为监控场景下的视频理解开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently achieved remarkable progress in general-purpose video understanding. However, their application to surveillance videos remains challenging due to the lack of large-scale domain-specific datasets and the limitation of passive observation from fixed viewpoints. In surveillance scenarios, critical visual evidence can be easily missed when targets are distant, small, occluded, or move beyond the current camera view. In this work, we introduce CamVLM, a new framework for Thinking with Cameras, which enables LVLMs to actively acquire visual evidence through dynamic viewpoint control rather than passively analyzing fixed video streams. We first construct CCTV-Anomaly, a large-scale surveillance video understanding dataset containing 14,459 videos across 10 anomaly categories, with detailed captions and event annotations. We further formulate viewpoint control as an active visual perception problem and build CamTrack-53K, an object-centric viewpoint trajectory dataset for learning camera actions. Moreover, we propose a reinforcement learning based viewpoint policy optimization framework, which models camera control as a sequential decision-making process and learns long-horizon observation strategies beyond supervised trajectory imitation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CamVLM achieves state-of-the-art performance under both passive observation and dynamic viewpoint settings, validating the effectiveness of active camera-based reasoning for surveillance video understanding. Our datasets, model, and code will be available at https://github.com/xiaozhang79/CamVLM .

</details>


### [93] [One MLLM, One Call: Efficient Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation via Spatial-Aware Waypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06476)
*Shiqi Pan,Qi Zheng,Hanqin Sun,Youjian Zhang,Daquan Feng,Xu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: O2C-Nav是一个高效的零样本视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）框架，每个决策步骤仅调用一次大型模型。它通过训练无关的结构化航点生成器和新型抽象表示（将候选航点投影为RGB图像上的视觉标记）为MLLM提供空间感知和显式记忆，再由快速行进法（FMM）规划器生成无碰撞路径，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上超越了现有零样本方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本VLN-CE方法要么依赖预训练的航点预测器，要么在每一步需要多次查询大型模型，导致推理延迟过高和计算开销巨大，难以满足实时部署需求。

Method: 提出O2C-Nav框架，包含：(1) 训练无关的结构化航点生成器；(2) 将稀疏的、历史感知的候选航点以视觉标记形式直接投影到RGB图像上的抽象表示方法；(3) 在每个决策步骤仅调用一次MLLM来选择航点或生成回退目标边界框；(4) 使用快速行进法（FMM）低级规划器将所选目标转换为可执行的碰撞自由路径。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上的大量实验表明，O2C-Nav优于当前最先进的零样本方法，展示了其在实际机器人实时部署中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 通过单次模型调用、结构化航点生成和视觉标记投影的策略，O2C-Nav有效解决了零样本VLN-CE中的推理延迟和计算开销问题，同时保持甚至超越了现有方法的导航性能，为实时机器人导航部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an embodied agent to navigate unseen environments by following natural language instructions. Current zero-shot VLN-CE methods either rely on pre-trained waypoint predictors or require multiple queries to large models per step. To address prohibitive inference latency and computational overhead, we propose O2C-Nav, an efficient zero-shot navigation framework that calls only a single large model once per decision step. Our approach introduces a training-free structured waypoint generator and a novel abstract representation that projects sparse, history-aware candidate waypoints directly onto RGB images as visual markers. The MLLM selects a waypoint or generates a fallback target bounding box at each step, while a low-level Fast Marching Method (FMM) planner converts the selected target into an executable collision-free path. This paradigm provides the model with concrete spatial perception and explicit memory while significantly reducing the visual processing load. Extensive evaluations on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks demonstrate that O2C-Nav outperforms current state-of-the-art zero-shot methods, highlighting its great potential for real-time robotic deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/kkpsq/O2C-Nav-Code.

</details>


### [94] [OracleZoom: On-Policy Self-Distillation Inspired Reference-Constrained Recursive Image Super Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06490)
*Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sourajit Saha,Shaswati Saha,Nobin Sarwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OracleZoom是一种基于on-policy蒸馏的参考约束递归超分辨率框架，通过携带最后的地面真值证据越过监督边界，在无法获取全尺度真实图像的深度放大场景中，实现跨七个数据集的最先进SR质量（平均CLIPIQA 0.713），同时显著减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归超分辨率在极端放大场景下，每个尺度都需要对应尺度的真实图像作为监督，但所需源分辨率随深度呈几何级增长，导致深层预测缺乏监督信号。这一监督边界问题严重限制了递归SR在深度放大时的性能与真实性。

Method: OracleZoom采用on-policy蒸馏启发的参考约束框架，在其自身轨迹上训练并携带最后可用的地面真值证据跨越监督边界。直接与跨尺度监督约束可验证内容，无参考质量目标引导未解决的细粒度细节，KL约束的预训练潜在先验限制质量驱动的漂移，EMA一致性稳定监督边界。

Result: 在七个数据集上实现跨缩放尺度的最先进SR质量，平均CLIPIQA达到0.713，在更深尺度上收益更大，并显著减少了幻觉现象。

Conclusion: OracleZoom有效解决了递归超分辨率中深层监督缺失的难题，在不依赖更深层真实图像的情况下实现了更高质量和更少幻觉的极端放大，为递归SR的深度扩展提供了可行的训练范式。

Abstract: Recursive Super-Resolution (SR) extends fixed-scale SR to extreme magnification by repeatedly feeding predictions back into the same model, analogous to zooming an image repeatedly. However, ground truth availability at every scale, especially at depth, remains challenging as the required source resolution grows geometrically, leaving deeper predictions unsupervised. We present OracleZoom, an on-policy distillation-inspired, reference-constrained framework that trains on its trajectory while carrying the last ground-truth evidence beyond the supervision boundary. Direct and cross-scale supervision constrain verifiable content, while a no-reference quality objective guides unresolved fine-scale detail. A KL-constrained pretrained latent prior limits quality-driven drift, while EMA consistency stabilizes the supervision boundary. Across seven datasets, OracleZoom achieves the state-of-the-art SR quality across zooming scales, averaging 0.713 CLIPIQA, with larger gains on deeper scales, while significantly reducing hallucinations. Code, data, and models are available at https://dipta007.github.io/OracleZoom/ .

</details>


### [95] [Diffuse2Seg: Diffusion Models Can Segment Anything Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06491)
*Christoph Hümmer,Joachim Sicking,Fabian Hüger,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diffuse2Seg利用文本到图像扩散模型的中间特征（自注意力表示）自动生成多粒度实例掩码，无需监督和重训练，在开放世界实体分割上超越现有标签生成器4.3-7.1 p.p.，并显著提升下游分割模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界实体分割需要大规模像素级标注，而像SAM依赖的SA-1B数据集（11M图像、超10亿掩码）标注成本极其高昂，限制该范式的扩展性。作者希望寻找无需昂贵标注就能生成高质量掩码的替代途径。

Method: 观察到文本到图像扩散模型在去噪过程中其自注意力表示已蕴含对象结构信息，且这些特征可迁移到感知任务中。据此提出Diffuse2Seg：在扩散模型的自注意力表示中传播网格状点提示（point prompts），以边缘保持的方式生成多粒度实例掩码，完全无需重训练或监督。

Result: 在五个域上比先前最优标签生成器在AR_1000上提升4.3-7.1个百分点；用生成的掩码训练实例分割模型，在"things"和"stuff+things"数据集上分别提升7.4和7.7个百分点，并在"stuff+things"上以AR_1000超出检测器类UnSAM 2.1个百分点；仅用5k标注图像进行半监督学习即可超越全监督对应模型。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的生成式中间表示可被有效利用作为自动掩码生成器，替代昂贵的人工标注流程，不仅推动了开放世界分割性能，还为半监督学习提供了强初始化，展现了生成模型在感知任务标注自举方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Open-world entity segmentation aims to predict masks for arbitrary objects across domains and at multiple granularities, from parts to whole objects. In this setting, SAM sets a strong standard: trained on SA-1B, comprising 11M images and over 1B carefully annotated masks, it achieves remarkable zero-shot performance. Collecting such labels is expensive and time-consuming, however, which limits how far this recipe can scale. Text-to-image diffusion models offer a way around this. Their intermediate features transfer well across perception tasks, and since object structure emerges as the model denoises a noise sample into an image conditioned on a text prompt, that structure is already encoded in these representations. They can therefore be exploited for open-world entity segmentation without retraining or supervision. Building on this observation, we introduce Diffuse2Seg, which repurposes generative diffusion models for automatic mask generation by propagating a grid of point prompts through their self-attention representations in an edge-preserving manner. Diffuse2Seg produces multi-granular instance masks and outperforms prior state-of-the-art label generators by 4.3-7.1 p.p. in AR_1000 across five domains. Training an instance segmentation model on these generated masks advances detector-free open-world segmentation by 7.4 and 7.7 p.p. on "things" and "stuff+things" datasets and surpasses the detector-based UnSAM on "stuff+things" by 2.1 p.p. in AR_1000. Finally, we show that a model trained on Diffuse2Seg labels provides a strong initialization for semi-supervised learning, outperforming its fully supervised counterpart with already 5k labeled images.

</details>


### [96] [CAM: Question Answering on Entity-Centric Videos with Continuous Extraction and Adaptive Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06504)
*Yizhou Tian,Zizhe Chen,Shiyuan Deng,Garry Yang,Zijie Dai,Luohao Pan,Hao Lin,Peiqi Yin,Xiao Yan,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAM是一个针对长视频问答的框架，通过知识图谱持续提取高层语义（实体与关系），并采用规划器-执行器-验证器流水线自适应组合多种检索方式（图谱遍历、视频回看、音频聆听）来获取细粒度细节，在三个基准上比SOTA方法最高提升23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频问答依赖从固定长度片段中提取独立记忆条目，无法捕获需要跨长时间段总结的高层语义（如人物性格和关系）；同时仅依赖相似性检索，可能遗漏问答所需的细粒度细节。

Method: CAM包含两个核心组件：（1）持续提取——将视频片段中的实体和关系存入知识图谱，当某个实体或关系的局部子图达到预设规模时对其进行总结以捕获高层语义；（2）自适应查询——支持知识图谱遍历、视频回看和音频聆听多种检索方式，通过规划器-执行器-验证器流水线根据问题意图自适应组合这些方法。

Result: 在三个基准上的评估表明，CAM优于现有SOTA基线，并将其准确率最高提升23个百分点。

Conclusion: CAM通过知识图谱驱动的持续提取和自适应多模态查询，有效解决了长视频问答中高层语义缺失和细粒度检索不足的问题，验证了该方法在长视频问答任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Memory facilitates question answering over long videos by extracting and retrieving facts to fit within the limited context windows of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs). Existing solutions typically extract independent memory entries from fixed-length video clips and thus cannot capture high-level semantics that need to be summarized over extended time periods, such as character traits and relations. Moreover, they rely solely on similarity-based retrieval and may fail to retrieve the fine-grained details required for question answering. To tackle these problems, we propose CAM, featuring continuous extraction for high-level semantics and adaptive querying for fine-grained details. In particular, CAM stores the entities and relations extracted from video clips in a knowledge graph. To capture the high-level semantics of each entity or relation, CAM summarizes the local subgraph of the target entity or relation once the subgraph reaches a predefined size. To retrieve the fine-grained details required for question answering, CAM supports multiple search methods, including knowledge graph traversal, video re-watching, and audio listening. It utilizes a planner-executor-verifier pipeline to adaptively compose these search methods according to question intent. Evaluations on three benchmarks show that CAM outperforms SOTA baselines and improves their accuracy by up to 23 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/Jake-Tian/CAM.

</details>


### [97] [A Cloud-Based Hybrid Model for Real-Time Detection of BRTA-Approved Licence Plates Using YOLO Tiny and Haar Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06507)
*Debashis Kar Suvra,Tahsina Farah Sanam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合YOLO Tiny深度学习模型与Haar-Cascade分类器的混合方法，用于孟加拉国车牌检测，并集成动态重训练管道以适应现实环境变化，在资源受限环境下实现高效准确的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国车牌检测面临本地化车牌复杂性及光照、遮挡、运动模糊、污垢等环境因素的独特挑战，传统方法效果不佳，亟需一种能适应不断变化的现实条件并适用于资源受限环境的检测方案。

Method: 采用YOLO Tiny深度学习模型与Haar-Cascade分类器相结合的混合检测框架；核心创新是集成动态重训练管道，使模型能够基于新遇到的数据持续优化，特别提升低置信度场景下的检测性能；同时构建了符合BRTA标准的公开车牌数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在检测准确性和计算效率上均优于传统模型，且在孟加拉国等资源受限环境中保持一致且稳定的检测性能。

Conclusion: 该混合方法与动态重训练机制有效解决了孟加拉国复杂环境下的车牌检测难题，验证了在资源受限场景中的实用价值，为该领域提供了新的技术路径和公开数据集资源。

Abstract: Accurate vehicle license plate detection is essential for applications such as intelligent transportation systems, toll collection, parking management, and law enforcement. In Bangladesh, this task presents distinct challenges due to the complexity of localized license plates and environmental factors like lighting, occlusion, motion blur, and obstructions such as dirt or mud. These challenges often render conventional methods ineffective. This paper introduces a novel hybrid approach, combining the YOLO Tiny deep learning model with the Haar-Cascade classifier, for enhanced detection and localization of Bengali license plates. A key innovation of our system is the integration of a dynamic retraining pipeline, which allows the model to adapt to evolving real-world conditions. This retraining mechanism significantly boosts performance in low-confidence scenarios by continuously improving the model's accuracy as new data is encountered. Additionally, a publicly accessible dataset of BRTA-compliant license plates, captured under diverse and challenging conditions, has been developed to support this approach. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach not only achieves superior detection accuracy and computational efficiency over conventional models but also ensures consistent performance in resource-constrained environments, particularly in Bangladesh.

</details>


### [98] [Selective Knowledge Control for Continual GUI Agent Learning over Application Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06530)
*Zirui Shang,Xin Shu,Yang Liu,Zhi Gao,Xinxiao Wu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激活条件的选择性知识控制方法，通过神经元级别的梯度操作实现GUI代理在持续学习中的知识保留，在不干扰旧任务知识的同时适应新应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI代理需要在不断演进的应用环境中持续学习，面临着灾难性遗忘与新知识获取之间的根本矛盾。新应用与旧应用既共享底层知识又包含特定知识，如何在不干扰历史知识的前提下适应新应用是一个关键挑战。

Method: 该方法维护一个紧凑的历史知识状态来保护保存先前知识的高激活MLP神经元。当新应用到来时，基于前向激活进行实时梯度手术：将受保护神经元分为两类——未激活神经元（持有特定知识）截断其梯度防止干扰，激活神经元（持有共享知识）通过正交投影保持稳定性同时允许适应。每个应用阶段结束后，新识别的关键神经元被合并到历史状态中。

Result: 在多应用顺序基准上的实证评估表明，该方法有效缓解了先前应用的灾难性遗忘，同时保持了对新应用的稳健适应能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的轻量级方法通过细粒度的神经元级梯度控制，在GUI代理持续学习场景中实现了选择性的知识保留与灵活的知识更新，兼顾了稳定性与可塑性。

Abstract: Continual learning is a crucial capability for Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents to adapt to evolving applications while retaining knowledge acquired from previous applications. Such application streams pose a challenging knowledge modeling problem: new applications often share underlying knowledge with past ones, yet also introduce specific knowledge that must not interfere with historical knowledge. In this paper, we propose activation-conditioned selective knowledge control, a lightweight method that achieves selective knowledge retention via neuron-level gradient manipulation. Our method maintains a compact historical knowledge state to protect highly activated MLP neurons that preserve previous knowledge. When a new application arrives, it performs real-time gradient surgery conditioned on forward activation. Concretely, the protected neurons are categorized into two types: unactivated neurons holding specific knowledge, whose gradients are truncated to prevent interference; and activated neurons holding shared knowledge, whose gradients are orthogonally projected to preserve stability while enabling adaptation. After each application stage, newly identified critical neurons are merged into the historical state for future learning. Empirical evaluations on multi-app sequential benchmark demonstrate that our method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on prior applications while sustaining robust adaptation to new ones.

</details>


### [99] [3DHarnessBench: Probing Agentic 3D-to-Code Capabilities of Frontier Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06535)
*Ling Liu,Bingchen Gong,Amal Dev Parakkat,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DHarnessBench基准，用于评估前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）将各种输入恢复为Blender Python代码形式的3D几何结构的智能体能力。该基准设计了四种逐渐增强的智能体探索设置（单视图、多视图、主动视觉和完整3D交互），发现所有模型在更丰富的函数调用访问下3D恢复能力显著提升，但提升幅度高度依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D恢复评估框架通常使用固定输入（如单张渲染图或文本描述）提示VLM，无法评估模型在主动探索、工具调用和自我纠错等智能体能力方面的表现，缺乏对前沿VLM在实际3D重建任务中智能体化能力的系统性评测。

Method: 设计了3DHarnessBench基准，包含四个层级递进的智能体设置：Single-view（单视图）、Multi-view（多视图）、Active Visual（任意视角访问）、Full 3D Interaction（通过Blender函数调用完整访问目标物体）。利用Blender MCP功能实现主动智能体探索，从视觉感知、主动推理、工具调用和自我纠错等多维度评估模型能力。

Result: 所有前沿模型的3D几何恢复能力随着函数调用访问的丰富程度增加而显著提升，但这种改进高度依赖具体模型，揭示了不同模型在智能体化3D到代码任务中能力存在显著不均。

Conclusion: 3DHarnessBench为3D几何恢复的智能体能力提供了可复现的评估基准，并计划发布基准、代码、输出及智能体轨迹以支持可重复的3D评估研究。

Abstract: We introduce 3DHarnessBench, a benchmark that evaluates the agentic ability of frontier vision-language models (VLMs) to recover 3D geometry as Blender Python code from a variety of inputs. Unlike previous frameworks that prompt the VLMs with a fixed input (e.g., a single rendering or a text description), 3DHarnessBench evaluates four separate harness settings that progressively enable active agentic exploration, facilitated by recent Blender MCP functionality. Our hierarchy from Single-view, Multi-view, Active Visual (arbitrary viewpoint access), and Full 3D Interaction (complete access to the target object through Blender function calls) probes the models' abilities in both visual perception and active inference, tool calling, and self-correction. We observe that the ability of all frontier models to recover 3D geometry improves significantly with richer function call access, although the improvements are strongly model-dependent, revealing highly uneven agentic 3D-to-code capabilities. We will release the benchmark, code, outputs, and agent trajectories for reproducible 3D evaluation.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Use Imagination: Progress-Conditioned Future Utilization for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06578)
*Yijie Zhu,Zitong Yu,Wei Li,Hui Ma,Wen Li,Rui Shao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProWAM，一种进度条件化的世界动作模型，通过显式利用执行进度作为中间表征，解决现有WAM在想象未来利用上的非均匀性问题，在多个基准上超过强VLA和WAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在利用想象未来时缺乏对执行进度的自适应调整，存在两类非均匀性：(i)跨进度层面，不同执行阶段对未来想象的需求不同；(ii)进度内层面，同一进度状态下不同未来潜变量的相关性存在异质性。这些问题可能导致预测线索分散或不可靠。

Method: ProWAM包含两个紧密耦合的组件：(1) 自监督双时态进度编码器（SS-DTPE），耦合短期动作-观测交互建模与长期循环进度聚合，获取可靠的执行进度表示；(2) 层次化进度条件想象调制（HPIM），在跨进度层面通过全局调制机制自适应调整未来利用，在进度内层面通过相关性机制区分每个进度状态中不同未来潜变量的作用。

Result: 大量实验表明，ProWAM在强VLA和WAM基线上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: ProWAM验证了将执行进度作为显式中间表征可有效改善WAM中想象未来的利用效率，解决未来效用非均匀性问题，为世界动作模型的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) extend Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by incorporating future visual dynamics into action generation. However, existing WAMs often utilize imagined futures with limited adaptation to evolving execution progress, potentially introducing distracting or unreliable predictive cues. This limitation arises from two empirically identified forms of non-uniformity in future utility: (i) at the inter-progress level, the utility of imagined futures varies across execution stages as control demands change; and (ii) at the intra-progress level, individual future latents exhibit heterogeneous relevance within the same progress state. To address these limitations, we propose ProWAM, a Progress-Conditioned World Action Model that introduces execution progress as an explicit intermediate representation for adaptive imagination utilization. ProWAM comprises two tightly coupled components: (1) To obtain a reliable representation of execution progress, we propose the Self-Supervised Dual-Temporal Progress Encoder (SS-DTPE). SS-DTPE couples short-term action-observation interaction modeling with long-term recurrent progress aggregation to capture recent execution feedback and accumulated task history. (2) Conditioned on the progress representation from SS-DTPE, we propose the Hierarchical Progress-Conditioned Imagination Modulation (HPIM) to adapt imagination utilization to execution progress. HPIM operates at two complementary levels: an inter-progress global modulation mechanism adapts future utilization across execution stages, while an intra-progress relevance mechanism differentiates individual future latents within each progress state. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA and WAM baselines.

</details>


### [101] [Reading Decoder Trajectories: Training-Free Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability for Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06581)
*Zhaoning Shi,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CQTR框架，通过反事实尺度干预激活冻结检测器中潜在的小目标知识，并利用解码器内部线索评估可靠性，无需训练或标注即可显著提升多种冻结检测器在多个数据集上的AP和APs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小目标检测方法大多假设冻结模型缺乏检测小目标的能力，因此通过多尺度训练、架构改造或参数自适应来改进。作者对此提出质疑，认为冻结模型中已存在小目标知识，只是未被激活且查询演化过程中不稳定，需要一种无需训练的方法来挖掘这些潜在能力。

Method: 提出CQTR（Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability）框架：(1) 通过反事实尺度干预激活冻结模型中潜在响应；(2) 从解码器内部的空间收敛性、语义持久性和跨尺度冲突三个维度评估候选可靠性；(3) 利用少量无标注训练子集为每个模型-数据流选择适当的修正机制，无需参数更新或目标域标注。

Result: 在9个冻结检测器×3个数据集的27种组合上，CQTR一致性地提升了平均精度(AP)和小目标平均精度(APs)。闭环分析进一步验证了尺度干预能激活潜在响应、轨迹证据能预测真实标注支持、无标注路由能选择更有效的分支。

Conclusion: CQTR将小目标检测的范式从外部尺度增强重新定义为对潜在尺度知识的激活与可靠性评估，挑战了"冻结模型缺乏小目标能力"的传统假设，为无需训练的检测器增强提供了新思路。

Abstract: Small-object detection remains challenging because limited pixels cause information loss and suppress the scale knowledge encoded in pretrained detectors. Existing approaches mainly improve representations through multiscale training, architecture redesign, or parameter adaptation, implicitly assuming that frozen models lack the required capability. We challenge this assumption and hypothesize that small-object knowledge already exists in frozen detectors but remains underactivated and unstable during query evolution. To test this hypothesis, we propose Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability (CQTR), a training-free framework that elicits latent responses through counterfactual scale interventions and interprets candidate reliability from decoder-internal spatial convergence, semantic persistence, and cross-scale conflicts. A small unlabeled training subset selects the appropriate correction mechanism for each model-data stream, without parameter updates or target-domain annotations. Across 27 combinations of nine frozen detectors and three datasets, CQTR consistently improves average precision (AP) and average precision for small objects (APs). Closed-loop analyses further show that scale intervention activates latent responses, trajectory evidence predicts ground-truth support, and unlabeled routing selects the more effective branch. CQTR therefore reframes small-object detection from external scale augmentation to the activation and reliability assessment of latent scale knowledge.

</details>


### [102] [Multi-History-Step SDE Inversion for Image Editing with Superior Regional Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06602)
*Haiyan Wei,Yunlong Wang,Huaibo Huang,Zhenan Sun,Kunbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIEdit，一个基于SDE反演的无训练图像编辑框架。通过预测-校正多历史步方案提升编辑质量与效率，缓解多条件噪声残差与梯度项之间的冲突以增强编辑稳定性与塑性，并引入Inversion-Time自动语义角度掩码（IASM）实现对未编辑区域的自动保护。同时构建了包含30个细粒度任务、1000余个图像-文本-掩码三元组的EditEval++评测基准，实验表明MIEdit在效果上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散SDE反演及无反演方法的无训练图像编辑技术虽然能够无需调参实现忠实重建，但仍存在编辑效率低、塑性有限、未编辑区域保持不准确等问题，亟需更高效且精确的编辑框架。

Method: 1) 提出预测-校正多历史步方案，实现更少步数下的高质量编辑；2) 缓解采样过程中多条件噪声残差与梯度项之间的异质性和冲突，提升大规模编辑下的稳定性与塑性；3) 提出IASM机制，利用无分类器引导在反演过程中自动生成语义角度掩码，无需额外用户输入即可施加区域约束。

Result: 构建了EditEval++综合评测基准（30个细粒度任务，1000余个图像-文本-掩码三元组），实验证明MIEdit在编辑质量上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MIEdit作为一种无训练的图像编辑框架，通过技术创新在编辑效率、塑性以及未编辑区域保真度上取得了全面改进，并提供了更全面的评测基准以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: In recent years, diffusion stochastic differential equation (SDE) inversion and inversion-free methods have become prevalent for training-free image editing, as they can achieve faithful reconstruction without tuning. However, existing approaches remain inefficient, exhibit limited plasticity, and struggle to accurately preserve unedited regions. To address these issues, we propose MIEdit, a training-free editing framework based on SDE inversion. MIEdit introduces a predictor-corrector multi-history-step scheme to achieve superior editing quality with fewer steps. We further mitigate heterogeneity and conflict between the multi-conditioned noise residuals and gradient terms during sampling, improving stability and editing plasticity under large edits. MIEdit also includes Inversion-Time Automatic Semantic Angle Masking (IASM); it leverages classifier-free guidance to automatically generate semantic angle masks during inversion and applies them throughout the sampling process for regional constraints, without extra user inputs. We additionally construct EditEval++ (30 fine-grained tasks, 1,000+ image-text-mask triplets) for comprehensive evaluation; experiments show that MIEdit outperforms state-of-the-art techniques. Project page: https://whywwwzzzg.github.io/MIEdit/.

</details>


### [103] [GAN-Blot: A Controllable Structure-Style Synthesis Benchmark for Western Blot Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06619)
