Method: 使用退化导向变换生成合成旧域图像，采用EfficientNetV2-M模型在身份不相交的训练/验证/测试集上进行评估，覆盖三个数据集分区和三个训练种子。比较了真实-合成混合训练与纯真实基线的效果，并考察了完全替换与部分补充两种策略，以及按比例缩放和固定300批次每轮两种训练调度方案。

Result: 完全替换真实历史图像使双向平均R@1从86.56%降至81.27%；在25%真实历史覆盖率下，合成补充提升R@1达3.69个百分点，50%时为2.92个百分点，75%时降至2.00个百分点，此时性能与完整真实数据参考相当。增加合成变体数量无一致改进，固定调度纯真实对照未复现这些提升。

Conclusion: 真实与合成观测数据具有互补性，合成老化不能完全再现真实旧域的变异性。合成数据补充在历史图像稀缺场景下是一种有效的增强策略，其贡献主要体现在扩展跨域身份覆盖范围上，并随真实历史覆盖率增加而递减。

Abstract: Cultural heritage collections often contain contemporary and historical visual records of the same physical object. Linking these records is difficult because corresponding images may differ in viewpoint, acquisition conditions, color reproduction, framing, resolution, and degradation, while genuine historical images are frequently scarce. This study investigates whether synthetically aged contemporary images can replace or complement missing historical training data in bidirectional instance-level retrieval. Synthetic old-domain images are generated using degradation-oriented transformations. An EfficientNetV2-M model is evaluated on identity-disjoint training, validation, and test sets across three dataset partitions and three training seeds. Mixed real-synthetic training is compared with real-only baselines using proportionally scaled and fixed 300-batch-per-epoch schedules. Complete replacement of genuine historical images reduced bidirectional mean R@1 from 86.56% to 81.27%, showing that synthetic aging does not reproduce the full genuine old-domain variability. Increasing the number of independently generated synthetic variants provided no consistent improvement. Under controlled scarcity, however, synthetic completion improved mean R@1 by 3.69 percentage points at 25% genuine historical coverage and by 2.92 points at 50%, relative to the proportionally scaled real-only baselines. At 75%, the gain decreased to 2.00 points, while performance remained comparable to the complete-real-data reference. Fixed-schedule real-only controls did not reproduce these improvements. The results indicate that genuine and synthetic observations are complementary. Synthetic completion primarily benefits retrieval by extending cross-domain identity coverage rather than by increasing training exposure, with its contribution gradually decreasing as genuine historical coverage increases.

</details>


### [277] [AXS-Net: Interpretable Deep Unfolding for Hyperspectral Image Denoising via Spectral Basis Unmixing and Structured Noise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08777)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Zheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AXS-Net，一种将高光谱图像去噪建模为低秩光谱子空间重构+结构化稀疏噪声+残差高斯噪声的优化问题，并通过展开K阶段交替近端点框架进行求解的网络，在ICVL、CAVE和Harvard数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度去噪网络直接将干净图像回归，容易将信号与结构化噪声纠缠在一起，缺乏对混合噪声（带相关高斯扰动、条纹、死线、脉冲噪声）的显式建模与可解释性。

Method: 将HSI去噪形式化为Y=AX+S+N的优化问题（A为光谱子空间/端元，X为丰度，S为结构化稀疏噪声，N为残差高斯噪声），并将其展开为K阶段交替近端点框架AXS-Net。每阶段包含：解析光谱基梯度步、处理丰度系数的SSX-Block近端算子、以及带列一致性和稀疏先验的结构化残差S-Block近端算子。

Result: 在ICVL、CAVE、Harvard数据集和五种噪声配置下，AXS-Net在域内精度上表现强，并具有竞争力的零样本迁移能力。在ICVL和Harvard上所有五种噪声场景均获得一致增益。恢复的结构化噪声与合成参考高度吻合，恢复的光谱基平滑且按波段有序排列。

Conclusion: 通过将优化展开与深度近端算子结合，AXS-Net不仅实现了高精度去噪，还提供了可解释的端元、丰度图和结构化噪声估计，验证了显式噪声分解建模在HSI去噪中的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images (HSIs) are often degraded by mixed noise, including band-dependent Gaussian perturbations and structured artifacts such as stripes, dead-lines, and impulse noise. Most deep denoisers regress the clean image directly, entangling signal and structured noise. We instead model HSI denoising as $\Y=\A\X+\Snoise+\Nnoise$, where $\A\X$ is a low-rank spectral-subspace (unmixing) reconstruction, $\Snoise$ is structured sparse noise and $\Nnoise$ is residual Gaussian noise. The resulting regularized optimization problem is unrolled into AXS-Net, a $K$-stage alternating proximal-point framework. Each stage combines an analytic spectral-basis gradient step, an SSX-Block proximal operator for abundance coefficients, and an SBlock proximal operator for the structured residual with column-consistent and sparse priors. This optimization correspondence exposes interpretable endmembers, abundance maps, and structured-noise estimates. Across ICVL, CAVE, and Harvard datasets and five noise configurations, the proposed AXS-Net achieves strong in-domain accuracy and competitive zero-shot transfer, with consistent gains across all five noise regimes on ICVL and Harvard. The recovered structured-noise closely follows the synthetic reference, and the recovered spectral basis is smooth and band-ordered rather than an arbitrary set of latent channels.

</details>


### [278] [Interpretable Hyperspectral Unmixing Framework with Fixed Endmember Prior and Structured Residual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08786)
*Ziyi Guan,Jianping Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的分步式高光谱解混框架（I-HyperSU），在固定端元先验下将解混显式分解为固定端元矩阵、丰度块和结构残差精化块三个环节。通过FISTA求解丰度并引入软惩罚近似满足和为一约束，同时利用低秩SVD和轻量深度图像先验(DIP)对结构残差进行精化。实验表明该方法在不完美端元先验下优于硬单纯形投影，显著降低重构误差且不影响丰度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱解混流程中，端元先被识别并固定，再用其估计丰度。当固定端元不准确时，由光照变化、传感器伪影或物质边界引起的空间结构化失配会被错误地归入丰度变量，导致分解不稳定。现有方法缺乏对这种结构化失配与丰度之间交互的显式建模和解释。

Method: 提出I-HyperSU框架，将解混问题显式分解为三个环节：(1) 固定端元矩阵A；(2) 丰度块X，采用FISTA求解，结合非负性、稀疏增强和软惩罚近似满足和为一约束；(3) 结构残差精化块S，联合低秩SVD结构正则化与轻量深度图像先验(DIP)来精化结构化残差。分步设计使丰度与残差间的交互透明可解释。

Result: 在Samson、Urban和Jasper Ridge数据集上进行实验。在固定且不完美的端元先验下，软丰度松弛一致优于硬单纯形投影。在默认N-FINDR端元先验下，与固定A的UCLS基线相比，联合重构误差降低61.7%~69.5%，同时丰度RMSE几乎不变。Urban数据上，重构SAM从X-only模型的5.99°降至完整模型的1.92°。

Conclusion: 残差精化分支能够有效吸收固定端元先验下的结构化模型失配，而不损害丰度估计的准确性。通过将丰度估计与残差精化显式分离，框架提升了高光谱解混的稳定性和可解释性，为模块化感知管线中受固定端元限制的场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing decomposes mixed pixels into material endmembers and their abundances from contiguous spectral observations. In modular sensing pipelines, endmembers are often first identified and then treated as fixed during abundance estimation. When this fixed endmember prior is inaccurate, spatially structured mismatch arising from illumination changes, sensor artifacts, or material boundaries may be incorrectly captured by the abundance variables, leading to unstable decompositions. This study presents an interpretable stage-wise hyperspectral unmixing framework (I-HyperSU) under fixed endmember priors, which is explicitly decomposed into a fixed endmember matrix $\mathbf{A}$, an abundance block $\mathbf{X}$, and a structural residual refinement block $\mathbf{S}$. The X-block estimates abundances using FISTA with nonnegativity and sparsity enhancement, and a soft penalty that approximately enforces sum-to-one constraints. The S-block jointly applies low-rank SVD structural regularization and a lightweight deep image prior (DIP) to refine structured residuals. This staged design makes the interaction between abundance and residual components transparent and interpretable. Experiments on Samson, Urban, and Jasper Ridge datasets demonstrate that, under fixed and imperfect endmember priors, soft abundance relaxation consistently outperforms hard simplex projection. Under the default N-FINDR endmember prior, the proposed framework reduces the joint reconstruction error by 61.7\%--69.5\% compared with a fixed-$\mathbf{A}$ UCLS baseline, while keeping the abundance RMSE nearly unchanged, indicating that the residual refinement branch accounts for structured model mismatch without degrading the abundance estimates. For example, on Urban, the reconstruction SAM decreases from $5.99^\circ$ for the X-only model to $1.92^\circ$ for the full model.

</details>


### [279] [Hi-FLoop: Hierarchical State-Feedback Loops for Multi-Timescale World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08796)
*Rx Fan,Zhan H*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HI-FLOOP，一个分支一致的多时间尺度状态反馈框架用于多智能体交通仿真，通过8秒目标层、2秒交互层、1秒控制层的分层设计，配合每0.5秒的前缀反馈机制和前缀冻结的A-to-B级联恢复策略，在H-D公开验证集上取得Overall 0.689987的S1成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时域闭环交通仿真方法通常从初始场景直接展开长未来，并将意图推理、交互建模和运动生成进行整体式求解，导致跨时间尺度的一致性和对生成状态的适应性不足。

Method: HI-FLOOP采用三层时间尺度分支：8秒Goal层锚定意图、2秒Preview层协调交互、1秒Control层生成物理运动；8个场景级World代表联合假设供所有智能体共享；每0.5秒仅将已执行的前缀作为事实反馈；JPI机制通过稀疏有向未来图和冲突概率门控交互精化；前缀冻结的A-to-B级联仅传递类型化物理状态和分支索引（不含潜状态）实现生成状态恢复。

Result: 在955个场景的完整H-D公开验证集上，单次S1运行Overall得分为0.689987；在agent-centric oracle评估下，oracle-minADE@8在8秒时域为1.196636米，6秒时域为0.526米。

Conclusion: HI-FLOOP通过分支一致的多时间尺度分解和状态反馈机制，有效增强了长时域仿真中跨尺度一致性与对生成状态的适应能力，取得了具有竞争力的官方评估成绩。

Abstract: Multi-agent traffic simulation seeks diverse, coordinated, and physically realistic futures from maps and observed history. Long-horizon closed-loop generation must reconcile multiple decision time scales while its context evolves with generated states. Existing methods often unfold long futures from the initial scene and resolve intent, interaction, and motion monolithically, weakening cross-scale consistency and adaptation. We present HI-FLOOP, a branch-consistent multi-timescale state-feedback framework. Eight scene-level Worlds represent joint hypotheses, and all agents share the selected World identity throughout an 8-second rollout. Within the branch, an 8-second Goal anchors intent, a 2-second Preview coordinates interactions, and 1-second Control produces physical motion. Every 0.5-second commit feeds back only its executed prefix as new facts, while unexecuted hypotheses never enter factual memory. Joint Preview Interaction (JPI) induces a sparse directed future graph from Preview and uses conflict probabilities and signed arrival-time differences to gate interaction refinement. For generated-state recovery, a prefix-frozen A-to-B cascade lets frozen Model A generate 0-1 seconds, then transfers typed physical state, admissible context, and the branch index, but no latent state, to an independent Model B for re-encoding and 1-2-second recovery. On the full H-D public-validation split of 955 scenarios, one complete S1 run yields an Overall score of 0.689987 with the official evaluator. Under agent-centric oracle evaluation, HI-FLOOP achieves oracle-minADE@8 of 1.196636 m over the 8-second horizon and 0.526 m over the 6-second horizon.

</details>


### [280] [Evaluation Principles for MRI-MRA Registration in Trigeminal Neuralgia: An ROI-Centered Neurovascular Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08805)
*Xupeng Zhang,Xihang Wang,Michael Xie,Haoyuan Liang,Hau Ern Lien,Oishika Das,James Feghali,Risheng Xu,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对三叉神经痛（TN）术前评估中MRI与TOF-MRA影像融合配准问题，提出以ROI为中心、血管感知、对比度和视野感知的评估框架。基于149例患者构建基准数据集，评估六种代表性配准流程后发现：传统全脑配准评估方法具有误导性，各评估指标间无法协同排序，可变形精化相比仿射配准仅带来视野相关的微弱优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TN术前评估需要MRI与MRA融合以可视化神经血管压迫，但临床相关目标仅为细小的三叉神经ROI区域，传统全脑配准评估准则无法准确反映该临床任务的配准质量，缺乏针对性的评估基准与方法论。

Method: 将TN MRI-MRA融合定义为一个以ROI为中心的神经血管配准-评估问题，从149例患者（含临床医生标注的双侧三叉神经ROI）构建基准数据集；对六种代表性配准流程采用局部图像指标、分割衍生的血管定位指标、预测体素分析以及对比度和视野分层比较进行系统评估。

Result: 传统评估汇总结果具有误导性：局部图像相似度、血管-背景可分性和下游血管定位不能对方法进行一致排序；单侧血管距离受部分标注下预测血管范围的影响显著；局部MRA对比度决定了血管可分性指标的有效时机；可变形精化相对仿射对齐仅提供微小且依赖视野的改进；读者评审发现局部有利的血管距离可能与全局不合理的配准并存。

Conclusion: TN MRI-MRA配准应被视为一个局部化的、血管感知的、对比度敏感且视野感知的可视化任务来进行评估，而非作为通用的多模态脑部配准问题对待，并已公开代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Preoperative evaluation of trigeminal neuralgia (TN) often requires joint interpretation of structural MRI, which depicts the trigeminal nerve and surrounding cisternal anatomy, and time-of-flight MRA, which highlights vascular structures. Although MRI-MRA fusion is clinically attractive for visualizing neurovascular compression, this task is poorly captured by conventional whole-brain registration evaluation because the clinically relevant target is a small trigeminal ROI, vessel annotations are partial and clinically focused, local TOF-MRA contrast is variable, and field-of-view mismatch can limit deformable alignment. We formulate TN MRI-MRA fusion as an ROI-centered neurovascular registration-evaluation problem and construct a benchmark from 149 patients with clinician-annotated bilateral trigeminal ROIs. Six representative registration pipelines were evaluated using local image-based metrics, segmentation-derived vessel-localization metrics, prediction-volume analysis, and contrast- and FOV-stratified comparisons. Conventional evaluation summaries were often misleading: local image similarity, vessel-background separability, and downstream vessel localization did not co-rank methods; one-sided vessel distances were strongly affected by predicted vessel extent under partial annotations; and local MRA contrast determined when vessel-separability metrics were informative. Deformable refinement provided only a small, FOV-dependent benefit over affine alignment, while reader review showed that locally favorable vessel distances could coexist with globally implausible registrations. These findings indicate that TN MRI-MRA registration should be evaluated as a local, vessel-aware, contrast-sensitive, and FOV-aware visualization task rather than as generic multimodal brain registration. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/TN-Reg-Benchmark.

</details>


### [281] [ArmPoser: Real-Time, Calibration-Free Arm Pose Estimation from Smartwatch IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08806)
*Bishnu Dev,Vasco Xu,Xi-Aan Loh,Chenfeng Gao,Henry Hoffmann,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArmPoser是一个基于单智能手表IMU的无校准手臂姿态估计系统，直接在消费级智能手表原生参考坐标系中训练模型，消除了坐标变换、显式对齐和骨骼偏移校准的需求，并包含佩戴配置模块来推断前臂正反面放置与表冠朝向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能手表的手臂姿态估计方法依赖校准姿态和预处理流程，将原始IMU测量转化为标准化训练格式，这阻碍了日常场景部署，且因校准不完善和传感器漂移引入误差。

Method: 核心贡献是直接在消费级智能手表固有的参考坐标系中训练模型，使学习过程与设备实际产出的IMU数据一致。此外引入物理约束下的手表放置位置和手臂形态变化以增强训练鲁棒性，并设计佩戴配置模块自动推断前臂前/后放置及表冠朝向。

Result: 在公共基准和一项包含10名参与者、30个活动、使用watchOS和Android智能手表的研究中评估，ArmPoser在无需任何用户校准的情况下达到或超越校准基线的性能。

Conclusion: ArmPoser证明了直接在设备原生坐标系下训练可以消除对手动校准的依赖，同时保持甚至超越传统校准方法的姿态估计精度，有望推动智能手表姿态估计在真实日常场景中的普及。

Abstract: Arm pose estimation enables applications in fitness, extended reality input, rehabilitation, and life logging. Prior smartwatch-based approaches rely on calibration poses and preprocessing pipelines that transform raw IMU measurements into standardized training formats. These steps hinder deployment in everyday settings and introduce errors due to imperfect calibration and sensor drift. We present ArmPoser, a calibration-free arm pose estimation system using a single smartwatch IMU. Our central contribution is training models directly in the reference frame native to consumer smartwatches, aligning learning with how IMU data is produced by deployed devices. By operating on device-native axes, ArmPoser removes the need for coordinate transformations, explicit alignment, and bone-offset calibration used in prior work. We further augment training with physically grounded variations in watch placement and arm morphology to account for user-specific variability. ArmPoser also includes a wear-configuration module that infers anterior or posterior forearm placement and crown orientation. We evaluate pose estimation on public benchmarks and on a 10-participant, 30-activity study using watchOS and Android smartwatches, where ArmPoser matches or exceeds calibrated baselines without any user calibration.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Gait: Person Identification from Millimeter-Wave Point Clouds Across Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08818)
*Xilai Wang,Zixiong Han,Saad Rhanmouni,Chenzhe Zhao,Yunze Lu,Miodrag Bolic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以活动为条件的毫米波点云人员识别框架（mm-ADL），通过活动识别路由器将每个片段路由到特定活动的身份专家模型，超越了传统仅依赖步态的识别方法。在7种日常生活活动（ADLs）的11人数据集上，闭集识别准确率从62.1%提升至68.0%，双人重识别mAP从57.2%提升至75.4%，Rank-1从59.1%提升至82.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内行走通常时间短且常被打断，仅依赖步态进行毫米波点云人员识别具有局限性。日常活动（ADLs）可能提供补充的身份信息，但不同活动的时空特征差异大，如何有效利用活动上下文来提升身份识别性能是核心研究动机。

Method: 提出一个活动条件化框架，由人类活动识别路由器将每个点云片段分配给特化的活动身份专家模型，实现为监督式混合专家（MoE）架构，采用双流静态-动态PointNet（DS-SDPNet）结合时间聚合的空间结构与帧间动态信息。

Result: 在闭集识别（ID）中，硬路由将准确率从62.1%提升至68.0%；在双人重识别（ReID）设置中，硬路由使mAP从57.2%提升至75.4%，Rank-1从59.1%提升至82.1%。活动特定专家在匹配画廊划分下也优于共享嵌入。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，超越步态利用日常活动进行人员识别是可行的，且在受控室内条件下活动条件化对身份表示学习具有显著价值。

Abstract: Person identification from millimeter-wave (mmWave) point clouds has mainly relied on gait. Indoor walking, however, is often brief and interrupted, while other activities of daily living (ADLs) may provide complementary identity information. We investigate identification across seven ADLs using mm-ADL, a new point-cloud dataset collected from 11 subjects under a controlled protocol. This extension introduces heterogeneous states and transitions whose spatial and temporal characteristics vary with activity. We therefore study whether activity can provide useful context for learning identity representations. We propose an activity-conditioned framework in which a human activity recognition router dispatches each clip to an activity-specific identity expert. The framework is implemented as a supervised mixture of experts, using a dual-stream static-dynamic PointNet (DS-SDPNet) to combine time-aggregated spatial structure with frame-to-frame information. We evaluate closed-set identification (ID) and subject-disjoint re-identification (ReID). With learned hard routing, ID accuracy increases from 62.1% to 68.0%. In a two-occupant ReID setting, hard routing increases mAP from 57.2% to 75.4% and Rank-1 accuracy from 59.1% to 82.1%. Under a matched gallery partition, activity-specific experts also outperform a shared embedding, showing that the gain extends beyond restricting the gallery. These results support the feasibility of using ADLs beyond gait for identification and the value of activity conditioning under controlled indoor conditions.

</details>


### [283] [Leveraging Visual and Geometric Priors for Metric-scale and Complete Vehicle Gaussian Reconstruction from Limited Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08841)
*Jinyu Miao,Jiusi Li,Yifei He,Miao Long,Kun Jiang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前馈式车辆资产重建方法，利用两种互补先验（视觉先验和几何先验），从车载相机拍摄的单侧稀疏观测中重建具有度量尺度的3D高斯车辆表示，解决了图像转3D方法无可靠尺度以及单侧观测导致的多视图重建不完整两个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真车辆资产对可控交通场景生成至关重要，尤其是合成罕见且安全关键的长尾场景。然而从车载图像重建可复用的车辆表示面临两大挑战：一是图像转3D生成方法缺乏可靠的度量尺度；二是车载相机通常仅观察到目标车辆的一侧，导致传统多视图重建在未观测区域不完整。

Method: 提出一种前馈式车辆资产重建方法：1）利用视觉基础模型作为视觉先验进行高斯初始化；2）通过可学习的编码器-解码器模块估计高斯属性；3）提出对称感知克隆策略，利用车辆的双边结构作为几何先验，直接在高斯空间补全未观测的一侧。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在车辆资产完整性和几何精度方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合视觉先验与几何先验，所提方法成功实现了从单侧稀疏观测中生成具有度量尺度且完整的3D高斯车辆表示，为交通场景中的长尾场景合成提供了高质量车辆资产支撑。

Abstract: High-fidelity vehicle assets are essential for controllable traffic scene generation, particularly for synthesizing rare and safety-critical long-tail scenarios. However, reconstructing a reusable vehicle representation from in-the-wild onboard images remains challenging for two reasons. First, image-to-3D generation methods generally produce models without reliable metric scale. Second, onboard cameras usually observe only one side of a target vehicle, making conventional multi-view reconstruction incomplete on unobserved regions. To solve these problems, we propose a feed-forward vehicle asset reconstruction method, which leverages two complementary priors to reconstruct 3D Gaussian representations for vehicles using sparse one-sided observations. To achieve metric-scale reconstruction, a visual foundation model is first utilized to serve as a visual prior for Gaussian initialization. The Gaussian attributes are then estimated by a learnable encoder-decoder module. A symmetry-aware cloning strategy is presented to complete the unobserved side directly in Gaussian space, which exploits the bilateral structure of vehicles as a geometric prior. Experiments on the public dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both vehicle asset completeness and geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [284] [DSE-VTG: Dual-Side Enhancement for Training-Free Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08850)
*Zhuo Cao,Bingqing Zhang,Sen Wang,Xue Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSE-VTG框架，通过视觉侧的多尺度相似度融合（MSF）和文本侧的查询级测试时适配（Q-TTA）双端增强，无需任务特定训练即可实现文本引导的视频时间定位（VTG），在多项基准上达到训练自由方法的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTG方法依赖密集时间标注和任务特定模型训练，成本高且易受分布偏移影响；已有的训练自由方法虽通过匹配预训练视觉-语言表示绕过训练，但面临两个瓶颈：帧级视觉编码忽略时间动态性，固定查询嵌入无法解决查询歧义。

Method: 提出DSE-VTG双端增强框架。视觉侧使用多尺度相似度融合（MSF），将帧级和片段级相似度组合为统一的时间感知相似度图谱；文本侧使用查询级测试时适配（Q-TTA），在测试阶段优化轻量级加性偏移量来调整查询嵌入以适应视频，无需微调主干网络或调用外部大语言模型。

Result: 在三个标准基准和两个OOD基准上达到训练自由方法的最优性能。在Charades-STA上比最强训练自由方法提高5.61个mIoU点；在分布偏移场景下，Charades-CG Novel-Word上达到50.86 mIoU，超越最强监督基线2.76个mIoU。

Conclusion: DSE-VTG通过同时解决视觉时间动态缺失和查询歧义两大信息瓶颈，在不进行任务特定训练的情况下实现了优越的VTG性能，尤其在分布偏移下表现强健，为训练自由的视频时间定位提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Text-guided Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) aims to localize the relevant segments in an untrimmed video based on text queries, yet collecting dense temporal annotations and training task-specific models remain costly and brittle under distribution shift. Recent training-free VTG approaches mitigate this issue by directly matching pretrained vision-language representations, but they still face two fundamental information bottlenecks: frame-wise visual encoding overlooks temporal dynamics, while fixed query embeddings cannot resolve query ambiguity. To address these issues, we propose DSE-VTG, a \underline{D}ual-\underline{S}ide \underline{E}nhancement framework that addresses both without any task-specific training. On the visual side, Multi-scale Similarity Fusion (MSF) combines frame- and clip-level similarities into a unified, temporally aware similarity profile. On the textual side, Query-level Test-Time Adaptation (Q-TTA) optimizes a lightweight additive offset to adapt the query embedding to the video at test time, without finetuning the backbone or calling external large language models. Extensive experiments on three standard and two OOD benchmarks show that DSE-VTG achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods. On Charades-STA, it improves mIoU over the strongest prior training-free method by 5.61 points. Under distribution shift, DSE-VTG reaches 50.86 mIoU on Charades-CG Novel-Word, surpassing the strongest supervised baseline by 2.76 mIoU. Our code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [285] [SeGDeP: Semantic- and Geometric-Aware Decoupled Prompts for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08867)
*Linnan Zhao,Xu Liu,Lingling Li,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Wenping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeGDeP提出了一种"what-where"显式分离的推理分割框架，将语义提示分支与独立的几何投影路径解耦，结合GDPO训练策略，在RefCOCO和ReasonSeg上取得SOTA性能，仅微调0.38%参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM分割模型将语义识别与空间定位信号混叠在单一上下文中，导致两类监督信号无法区分，且失败原因难以归因于目标理解还是定位错误。

Method: 构建语义/几何双通道显式接口：语义提示分支与DETR预测框的独立几何路径共同条件化SAM 3掩码解码器；采用组奖励解耦策略优化（GDPO）分别平衡格式、box-IoU和mask-IoU三类反馈。

Result: SeGDeP-4B在8个RefCOCO系列划分上达到82.7平均cIoU，在ReasonSeg验证/测试集上达到66.0/59.6 gIoU，仅通过LoRA适配Qwen3-VL参数的0.38%。阶段式消融和梯度诊断验证了两路径的互补专业化。

Conclusion: 显式分离语义与几何路径能有效解耦推理分割中的失败归因，使两条路径各自发展互补的专业能力，而非重复学习相同表征。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation converts an implicit linguistic conclusion into a precise mask, requiring both semantic identification and spatial grounding. Existing MLLM-segmenter interfaces either use a special trigger or compress both signals into one context, although they receive different supervision and fail differently. This coupling obscures whether a failure arises from target interpretation or from localization. We present SeGDeP, an explicit what-where interface. A semantic prompt branch and an independent geometric projection path transform resolved MLLM states into semantic features and a DETR-predicted box, which jointly condition a SAM 3 mask decoder. Training first aligns this executable interface, then uses group reward-decoupled policy optimization (GDPO) to balance format, box-IoU, and mask-IoU feedback. SeGDeP-4B reaches 82.7 average cIoU over eight RefCOCO-family splits and 66.0/59.6 gIoU on ReasonSeg val/test while adapting only 0.38% of Qwen3-VL parameters through LoRA. Controlled stage-wise ablations, gradient diagnostics, and prompt interventions further show that the two paths develop complementary semantic and geometric specialization rather than duplicating the same evidence.

</details>


### [286] [Medical AI Encodes a "Feeling of Error": Verifying Cancer Segmentation via Internal Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08879)
*Mengmeng Ma,Yunxiang Peng,Tang Li,Lu Lin,Binsheng Zhao,Oguz Akin,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用机制可解释性工具（稀疏自编码器）从癌症分割模型的内部激活中提取"错误感"（FOE）信号，通过概念激活特征训练分类器实现高精度的失败检测和错误原因解释，超越了传统基于输出置信度的失败检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症分割模型可能静默失败，生成看似合理但错误的掩码，导致漏诊或误检。现有基于输出层面的置信度/不确定性方法无法揭示失败原因，且存在灵敏度与分割质量之间的权衡矛盾。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将模型内部神经激活分解为人类可解释的概念字典，比较成功与失败分割案例的潜在签名差异（失败时激活概念更少且幅度更低），并在概念激活上训练分类器进行失败检测。

Result: 在前列腺癌、胰腺癌和脑癌分割实验中，该方法在失败检测方面优于基于输出的方法，同时保持了分割质量。

Conclusion: 模型内部确实存在类似人类"错误感"的信号，通过机制可解释性分析可以有效利用该信号进行失败预测并获得错误解释。

Abstract: Cancer segmentation models can fail silently, generating plausible but incorrect masks that risk missed findings or unnecessary biopsies. A critical question arises: Do AI models "know" when they are wrong, and if so, can we use the signal to predict their own failures? Humans do have a "Feeling of Error" (FOE): a spontaneous sense of unease that flags a potential error during thinking. We investigate whether cancer segmentation models exhibit an analogous internal signal. Unlike output-level cues (e.g., prediction confidence or uncertainty), which offer no insight into why a failure occurs and suffer from a sensitivity-quality tradeoff where high detection sensitivity could degrade overall segmentation quality. We instead propose to capture the model's FOE from its inner workings. Using mechanistic interpretability tools, specifically Sparse Autoencoders, we decompose internal neural activations into a dictionary of human-interpretable concepts and show that failure cases exhibit a distinct latent signature: fewer active concepts with lower activation magnitudes compared to successful segmentation. By training a classifier on these concept activations, we achieve accurate failure detection along with explanations for the model's mistakes. Experiments on prostate, pancreatic, and brain cancer segmentation demonstrate that our approach outperforms output-based methods in failure detection while preserving segmentation quality.

</details>


### [287] [CoSA: Correlation-Guided Change A ttention with Learnable Residual Gating for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08914)
*Abdirashid Omar,Jonghyuk Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种数据高效的固定交通摄像头跨视角人行横道分割管线：首先在源域FPV图像上训练U-Net作为基线（93.05% IoU），然后在目标域CCTV上仅用201张手动标注图像微调得到88.91% IoU，再利用置信度过滤和连通域几何先验对5,926张无标签帧筛选出1,000张伪标签样本。论文关键贡献在于审计发现报告中的98.52% IoU实为伪标签内部一致性而非人工真值精度，并强调了伪标签评估必须与自训练标签隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定交通摄像头图像逐像素标注成本高昂，而从街景视角训练的模型在应用于高位CCTV视角时存在严重的视角和外观偏移。作者旨在探索一条数据高效的跨视角人行横道分割迁移管线。

Method: 采用两阶段流程：1）源域实验使用31.0M参数的定制U-Net在3,300张FPV图像上训练；2）目标域使用42.0M参数DeepLabV3-ResNet50在201张手标CCTV图像上训练并在40张验证图上选择模型；3）对5,926张无标签帧进行预测，以图像级置信度并结合最大连通域面积先验进行候选排序，选取top 1,000生成伪标签；4）对代码仓库进行审计核实实验设置有效性与评估分割。

Result: 源域U-Net在FPV测试集上达到93.05% IoU。目标域模型在40张手标验证图上达到88.91% IoU。筛选出的top 1,000候选帧平均置信度0.976、综合得分0.988。审计揭示报告中的98.52% IoU是在仅含教师伪标签的150张分割上测得的，且因目录布局错误导致数据加载器实际未包含人工标注样本。批量为1的FP32推理在512x512分辨率下达12.98ms/77.03 FPS。

Conclusion: 研究支持置信度与几何先验过滤在实际跨视角分割任务中的可行性，同时警示伪标签评估必须与自训练所用标签保持独立，否则会高估模型在真实人工标注上的表现。可辩护的目标域结果为88.91% IoU而非伪标签内部一致性指标。

Abstract: Pixel-level annotation of fixed traffic-camera imagery is expensive, while crosswalk models trained from street-level imagery face a substantial viewpoint and appearance shift when applied to elevated CCTV. We investigate a data-efficient target-domain pipeline using 241 manually annotated CCTV images and 5,926 unlabeled CCTV frames. A source-domain experiment trains a 31.0M-parameter custom U-Net on 3,300 first-person-view (FPV) images and obtains 93.05% IoU on its 330-image FPV test split. This result is a source baseline, not transferred performance: the released CCTV notebook instantiates a 42.0M-parameter DeepLabV3-ResNet50 from torchvision weights, and no compatible mapping from the U-Net checkpoint is implemented. Training on 201 manual CCTV images and selecting on 40 held-out manual masks yields 88.91% IoU. The model then predicts all unlabeled frames; image-level certainty and a largest-component area prior rank the candidates, and the top 1,000 attain mean certainty 0.976 and mean combined score 0.988. A repository audit shows that the reported second-stage 98.52% IoU was measured on a 150-image split containing only teacher-generated pseudo-masks. Because of a directory-layout mismatch, the executed combined-data loader found zero manual samples and split 1,000 pseudo-labeled samples into 850 training and 150 evaluation samples. We therefore report 98.52% as internal pseudo-label agreement rather than human-ground-truth accuracy. The defensible target-domain result is 88.91% IoU on the 40 manual validation images. Batch-one FP32 inference at 512 x 512 requires 12.98 ms, corresponding to 77.03 FPS, on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 48 GB GPU. These findings support the practicality of confidence-and-geometry filtering while also showing why pseudo-label evaluation must remain isolated from the labels used for self-training.

</details>


### [288] [EgoSIS: From Factorized Visual Ego-Transitions to Motion-Canonical Spatial Evidence for UAV Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08938)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Mingxuan Cui,Yilin Sun,Hang Zhang,Jianhua Zhu,Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoSIS，一种无姿态适配器，通过三阶段处理将RGB导出的双向光流转换为运动规范化的视觉证据，用于无人机视频问答，在SIS-Bench上达到89.9%感知、82.5%感知加记忆和76.2%整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视频问答需要将相机运动与场景变化分离，但仅基于RGB的多模态模型缺乏明确的稳定参考来实现这种分离，导致场景变化的识别困难。

Method: EgoSIS包含三个关键组件：(1) FVET——拟合鲁棒的图像平面变换并暴露运动、残差支持和可靠性因子；(2) ReTEM——使用可靠性加权更新维护有界历史，并在剪切或持续不确定时重新锚定；(3) EASE——将支撑视觉特征扭曲到每个片段的局部锚点，通过零初始化残差注入四个空间证据token，不改变Qwen的视觉token数量。

Result: 在SIS-Bench上，EgoSIS-8B获得89.9%感知准确率、82.5%感知加记忆准确率和76.2%整体准确率，最大增益集中在自我意识感知和记忆方面。

Conclusion: 该适配器在光流与空间推理之间提供了一个可解释的接口，有效解决了无人机视频问答中相机运动与场景变化分离的关键挑战。

Abstract: UAV video question answering requires separating camera motion from changes in the scene, but RGB-only multimodal models receive no explicit, stable reference for that separation. We present EgoSIS, a pose-free adapter that converts RGB-derived bidirectional flow into motion-canonical visual evidence in three stages. Factorized Visual Ego-Transitions (FVET) fits a robust image-plane transition and exposes motion, residual-support, and reliability factors. Reliability-Gated Ego-Transition Memory (ReTEM) uses reliability-weighted updates for a bounded history and re-anchors it at cuts or sustained uncertainty. Ego-Aligned Spatial Evidence (EASE) warps supported visual features into each segment's local anchor and injects four spatial evidence tokens per visual slice through zero-initialized residuals, without changing Qwen's visual-token count. On SIS-Bench, EgoSIS-8B obtains 89.9\% perception, 82.5\% perception-plus-memory, and 76.2\% overall accuracy, with the largest gains concentrated in self-awareness perception and memory. The adapter thus provides an interpretable interface between optical flow and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [289] [Prior-free relative 6D pose estimation of multiple object instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08949)
*Behdad Khodabandehloo,Andrea Caraffa,Davide Boscaini,Fabio Poiesi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无先验相对6D位姿估计的新任务与解决方案PROSE，通过多模态基础特征找到未知物体实例间的粗对应，利用循环一致性精化后估计任意实例对的相对6D位姿，无需CAD模型、模板、参考图像或训练，并在新基准PRENCH上超越多种改编的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6D位姿估计方法已逐步减少对物体先验的依赖（从3D模型到多视角捕获再到单张参考图像），但现有方法仍假设已知场景中待定位的具体物体。作者旨在将该假设彻底去除，实现完全无先验的相对6D位姿估计，即在无CAD模型、模板或参考图像的情况下估计同一图像中未知物体的多个实例间的相对位姿。

Method: 提出PROSE方法：1）利用多模态基础特征在不同物体实例间寻找粗对应关系，无需训练；2）通过在实例元组上施加循环一致性约束来精化对应关系；3）利用全局一致的对应关系估计任意实例对之间的相对6D位姿。同时设计PRENCH基准，基于三个多实例BOP数据集构建并加入任务特定元数据以支持系统评估。

Result: PROSE在无任务特定监督且不需要额外学习组件的条件下，一致性地超越了由现有单图像SOTA方法改编得到的多个基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将6D位姿估计推向完全无先验的设置，证明仅靠多模态基础特征与循环一致性约束即可在没有训练、没有物体特定先验的情况下实现有效的相对6D位姿估计，为该方向提供了新的任务范式与基准。

Abstract: Object 6D pose estimation formulations have progressively reduced reliance on object-specific priors, evolving from explicit 3D models to multi-view object captures to single reference images. We take this progression to its extreme by introducing prior-free relative 6D pose estimation, which lifts the assumption of knowing which object is to be posed within the scene. This novel setting aims to estimate the relative poses of multiple instances of an unknown object within the same image, without requiring CAD models, templates, or reference images. We solve this by formulating a novel method (PROSE) that finds coarse correspondences between object instances using multimodal foundation features, thus requiring no training. We refine these correspondences by imposing cycle consistency across tuples of instances, and leverage the resulting globally consistent correspondences to estimate the relative 6D pose between any pair of instances. To enable systematic evaluation, we design a novel benchmark (PRENCH) built from three multi-instance BOP datasets and enriched with task-specific metadata. PROSE consistently outperforms baselines obtained by adapting state-of-the-art single-image methods to the proposed setting, while requiring neither task-specific supervision nor additional learned components. Project website: https://tev-fbk.github.io/PROSE/

</details>


### [290] [Concentrate After Imagination: Text-Conditioned Evidence Grounding for Partially Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08999)
*Shuaiqi Cheng,Siyu You,Yanbi Wu,Yuxi Chen,Jiahao Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种分数级别的证据验证算子，用于部分相关视频检索（PRVR），通过查询条件化的寄存器激活与证据校准机制，解决仅依赖最强局部响应导致的伪峰问题，在三个基准数据集上均取得最优SumR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRVR方法虽改进了局部表征、不确定性建模和全局上下文，但最终排序仍易过度信任最强的局部响应，导致恰好相似的片段产生无依据的峰值。作者将此问题识别为"查询无关的浓度瓶颈"（query-agnostic concentration bottleneck），即模型未能充分验证局部高分是否真正与查询语义相关。

Method: TRACE是一种分数级别的证据验证算子，核心流程包括：（1）根据查询激活全局视频中查询相关的寄存器；（2）将寄存器支持路由到帧级证据；（3）在执行局部时序选择前平滑边缘化查询-寄存器-帧的备选路径；（4）仅将证据作为查询条件化的残差校准，修正原始局部分数，而非在表征层面做特征融合。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions、Charades-STA和TVR三个基准上，TRACE均取得最佳SumR，相比DreamPRVR骨干网络分别提升1.2、1.1和1.5个点。消融实验、路由损坏分析、难负样本测试和跨骨干迁移实验均支持增益来自查询条件化的证据验证机制。

Conclusion: TRACE通过分数级别的证据验证有效缓解了PRVR中查询无关的浓度瓶颈问题。实验与分析表明其改进来源是查询条件化的证据校准而非通用的分数偏移，验证了该方法在局部证据验证方面的有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) retrieves untrimmed videos when queries describe only short moments. Although recent methods improve local representations, uncertainty modeling, and global context, final ranking often still trusts the strongest local response; a coincidentally similar fragment can therefore produce an unsupported peak. We identify this failure as the query-agnostic concentration bottleneck and propose TRACE, a score-level evidence verification operator for PRVR. Given a query and global video registers, TRACE activates query-relevant registers, routes their support to frame-level evidence, and smoothly marginalizes alternative query-to-register-to-frame paths before localized temporal selection. Unlike representation-level feature fusion, TRACE uses this evidence only as a query-conditioned residual calibration of the original local score. On ActivityNet Captions, Charades-STA, and TVR, TRACE achieves the best SumR on all three benchmarks and improves the DreamPRVR backbone by 1.2, 1.1, and 1.5 points, respectively. Ablation, routing-corruption, hard-negative, and cross-backbone transfer analyses support the interpretation that the gains arise from query-conditioned evidence verification rather than a generic score offset.

</details>


### [291] [DXPR: Depth-Based Vision-LiDAR Cross-Modal Place Recognition Using Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09005)
*Yungsoo Han,Youngseok Jang,Seungwon Roh,Jeongyeon Seo,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DXPR是一个基于深度图的跨模态地点识别框架，利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）将单目相机查询与LiDAR地图进行匹配，无需特定模态编码器。通过将相机图像和LiDAR扫描统一转换为深度图像表示，使用单一VFM骨干网络学习模态不变全局描述符，并引入几何感知重叠挖掘器提升度量学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预构建的LiDAR地图中仅使用相机进行鲁棒定位是机器人和自动驾驶的重要需求，但跨模态地点识别（CMPR）面临的挑战包括严重的季节、天气和光照变化，且现有的模态特定编码器方法难以有效实现跨模态匹配。

Method: 1) 将相机图像和LiDAR扫描统一转换为深度图像表示，使用单一VFM骨干网络和聚合头学习模态不变全局描述符；2) 引入几何感知重叠挖掘器：在跨模态尺度对齐相机和LiDAR深度后，进行正向扭曲计算像素级重叠分数；3) 利用重叠分数重新标注模糊对，并在多相似性损失中自适应调节正边界，避免对弱重叠视图的过拟合。

Result: 在KITTI里程计数据集上实现近乎完美的Recall@1，优于此前CMPR基线；在Boreas数据集上的序列内性能与强单模态基线（DINOv2-SALAD）相当，跨序列场景中表现明显更优；相比RangeBEV，在序列内和序列间评估中均表现更好。

Conclusion: DXPR通过在统一深度图像表示上采用VFM骨干网络，并结合几何感知重叠挖掘器，有效解决了跨模态地点识别中的模态差异问题，在多种极端环境条件下展现出强大的鲁棒性和性能优势。

Abstract: We present DXPR, a depth-based cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) framework that uses vision foundation models (VFMs) to match monocular camera queries against a LiDAR map without modality-specific encoders. This enables robots and autonomous vehicles to robustly localize using only cameras within pre-built LiDAR maps, even under severe seasonal, weather, and illumination changes. The key idea is to convert both camera images and LiDAR scans into a unified depth image representation so that a single VFM backbone with an aggregation head can learn modality-invariant global descriptors. To make pairwise metric learning faithful to scene geometry, we introduce a geometry-aware overlap miner: after cross-modal scale alignment of camera and LiDAR depth, we forward-warp measurements between views to compute a pixel-level overlap score. This score relabels ambiguous pairs and adaptively modulates the positive margin in a multi-similarity loss to avoid overfitting on weakly overlapping views. Extensive experiments on KITTI odometry and Boreas demonstrate strong performance and robustness across seasons, weather, and day/night. On KITTI, DXPR achieves near-perfect Recall@1 on most sequences and outperforms prior CMPR baselines. On Boreas, DXPR achieves intra-sequence performance on par with a strong single-modal baseline (DINOv2-SALAD), while showing clear improvements in the more challenging inter-sequence setting. Compared with RangeBEV, our method consistently performs better in both intra- and inter-sequence evaluations, demonstrating robustness under diverse seasonal and illumination changes.

</details>


### [292] [A Joint 2D-3D Statistical Shape Model for Orthopedic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09010)
*Florence Dell'Aniello Picard,Pranav Poudel,Nairouz Shehata,Frédéric Lavoie,Herve Lombaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合2D-3D统计形状模型（SSM），通过在共享潜空间中显式捕捉2D与3D分割之间的协变关系，直接从X光片恢复股骨3D形状，无需迭代的3D-to-2D投影匹配，推理速度约为基准方法的4倍（每例不到3秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从X光片进行三维股骨重建是不适定问题（投影丢失深度信息）。现有方法依赖3D统计形状模型加迭代投影匹配，不仅计算昂贵，而且将SSM限制在单一维度，未能充分利用2D观测与3D几何之间的统计关联。

Method: 构建一个联合2D-3D SSM，在共享潜空间中同时建模2D和3D分割的协变关系。训练时将2D与3D分割配准到公共3D模板及其对应的2D投影上，利用主成分分析（PCA）对生成的速度场（SVF）进行联合分解。推理时直接通过将2D潜坐标映射到3D PCA子空间来恢复3D形状，无需迭代投影匹配和推理时计算对应关系。

Result: 在NMDID数据集上的实验表明，所提出的联合2D-3D SSM在重建性能上优于广泛使用的仅3D的SSM基线，同时推理速度快约4倍，每个受试者不超过3秒。

Conclusion: 联合2D-3D统计形状建模能够直接从数据中学习2D到3D的映射，以更高的效率和精度实现从X光片的股骨三维重建，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Three-dimensional femoral reconstruction from radiographs supports surgical planning, implant sizing, and post-operative follow-up, but remains ill-posed as X-ray projections discard depth information. Existing methods often incorporate a 3D statistical shape model (SSM) as a shape prior to guide reconstructions toward anatomically plausible shapes, relying on iterative 3D-to-2D projection matching. Yet, these approaches are computationally expensive and constrain their SSM to a single dimensionality, leaving the statistical relationship between 2D observations and 3D geometry largely unexploited and unexplored. We instead propose a joint 2D-3D SSM that explicitly captures the co-variation between 2D and 3D segmentations in a shared latent space. During training, 2D and 3D segmentations are registered to a common 3D template and its corresponding 2D projections, and the resulting stationary velocity fields are jointly decomposed using principal component analysis (PCA). This joint modeling allows the 2D-to-3D mapping to be learned directly from data rather than computing correspondences at inference time. For unseen subjects, the 3D shape is recovered directly by lifting the 2D latent coordinates to the 3D PCA subspace, thereby eliminating the need for iterative 3D-to-2D projection. Experiments on NMDID demonstrate that the proposed joint 2D-3D SSM outperforms a widely-used 3D-only SSM baseline while achieving inference approximately 4 times faster, at under 3 seconds per subject. The code is available at: https://github.com/florence-dellaniello-picard/joint2d3d-ssm.

</details>


### [293] [PIC: Revisiting INR for Image Coding with Fast Encoding and Sub-Millisecond Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09020)
*Xiang Liu,Jinxiang Wang,Bin Chen,Zimo Liu,Mingyao Hong,Jiawei Li,Yaowei Wang,Shu-tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的前馈隐式神经表示（INR）图像编码架构PIC，在单次前向传播中计算INR网络所需的全部信息，实现了20 FPS的编码速度和2000 FPS的解码速度，在相近率失真性能下解码速度显著超越JPEG，是首个同时在RD性能和编解码速度上兼具实用性的基于学习的图像编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的INR图像压缩方法虽然在解码复杂度上具有显著优势，但实际应用受到编码速度慢和解码效率未被充分利用的阻碍，亟需一种兼顾编码速度和解码效率的实用方案。

Method: 提出PIC（Practical INR Image Codec）架构，采用前馈INR图像编码方式，在单次前向传播中计算INR网络所需的所有必要信息，同时实现了高度优化的解码器以充分利用解码效率。

Result: 编码速度达到20 FPS，解码速度达到2000 FPS，在相近的率失真性能下解码速度显著超越JPEG，成为首个在RD性能和编解码速度上同时优于或可比JPEG的基于学习的图像编解码器。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次展示了端到端学习图像编解码器能够在率失真性能和解码速度两方面同时超越或匹配JPEG，同时保持实用的编码速度，为INR图像压缩的实际部署迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) has achieved remarkable progress in novel view synthesis and image/video coding in recent years.Compared to conventional end-to-end image codecs, INR-based compressors demonstrate significant advantages in decoding complexity. However, their practical application has been hindered by the inferior encoding speed and underutilized decoding efficiency.In this work, we propose a feedforward INR image coding architecture, Practical INR Image Codec (PIC), that computes all the necessary information for INR network in a single forward pass, achieving an encoding speed of 20 FPS. Additionally, we implement a highly optimized decoder that reaches 2000 FPS decoding speed, significantly surpassing JPEG's performance at comparable rate-distortion (RD) performance. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first learning-based image codec that simultaneously outperforms or is comparable with JPEG in both RD performance and decoding speed while maintaining practical encoding speed. Code is available at https://github.com/actcwlf/PIC.

</details>


### [294] [Task-driven Processing with Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09025)
*Oleh Kolner,Thomas Ortner,Stanisław Woźniak,Angeliki Pantazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CF-GAP（Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception），一种任务驱动的视觉前端模块，通过一系列有限的局部"窥视"（glimpse）进行选择性聚焦，使现有实例检测器能够在高分辨率杂乱场景中高效定位目标，在多个基准上将AP提升最高20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型处理整幅图像，缺乏根据任务选择性放大关注区域的能力。在实例检测等任务中，高分辨率杂乱场景下的关键细节常因图像缩放而丢失，导致检测性能下降。

Method: CF-GAP作为任务驱动的前端模块，通过由粗到细的策略，利用任务信息迭代引导一系列有限的局部窥视序列，逐步精化对最相关区域的聚焦，再由下游实例检测器对这些局部区域进行高分辨率处理。

Result: 在HR-InsDet和Robotools基准上，CF-GAP使多种最先进的实例检测器平均精度（AP）提升最高达20%，并能使轻量级检测器性能超越其更大的同类模型。

Conclusion: 通过避免全图像处理并消除无关的干扰信息，基于任务驱动的选择性聚焦方法能够显著提升实例检测在高分辨率杂乱场景中的性能，同时具有更好的计算效率。

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision models process images in their entirety, lacking the ability to selectively zoom in on relevant regions. This limitation is particularly acute in scenarios where processing must be conditioned on a specific task - such as instance detection, which requires localizing a specific object in a high-resolution, cluttered scene. In such settings, critical details are easily lost as images are often resized to match the model dimensions and computational constraints. We introduce Coarse-to-Fine Glimpse-based Active Perception (CF-GAP), a task-driven front-end that enhances high-resolution processing of existing instance detectors. CF-GAP selectively directs a sequence of limited view glimpses across the scene, utilizing task information to iteratively refine focus on the most relevant regions. These localized regions are then processed at high resolution by a downstream instance detector. By avoiding full-image processing and eliminating irrelevant confounding information, CF-GAP improves Average Precision (AP) by up to 20% across various state-of-the-art instance detectors on the HR-InsDet and Robotools benchmarks, while further enabling lightweight detectors to outperform their larger counterparts.

</details>


### [295] ["World Knowledge" in the Weights: Reading Concept Circuits of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09055)
*Yanlin Chen,Tang Li,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用跨层转码器（CLTs）从视觉Transformer（ViTs）中提取"概念电路"——有向图结构，其中节点为稀疏可解释概念，边表示概念跨层交互。方法提供全局概念电路（输入不变，反映模型内嵌的世界知识）和实例概念电路（输入依赖，用于示例级解释），并在虚假相关性发现与消除、模型对比三个任务上展示效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer在众多视觉任务上表现优异，但其内部如何表征世界结构仍不清晰。现有可解释性研究多关注单层或单神经元，缺乏对跨层概念交互的系统性理解。

Method: 利用Cross-Layer Transcoders（CLTs）从ViT权重中重构两个互补视图：1）全局概念电路——直接从学习到的跨层权重中恢复，输入不变，捕获可复用的世界知识；2）实例概念电路——依赖具体输入，识别人预测实际使用的概念与路径，支持示例级解释。

Result: 在自动发现虚假相关性、去除虚假相关性（Waterbird数据集上超过现有方法11.0%）以及不同基础模型（如CLIP vs DINO）概念电路对比三个任务中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 概念电路为理解ViT内部世界结构提供了双视角框架，既能揭示模型中可复用的概念知识，又能支撑实例级可解释与模型操纵，并为不同预训练范式下的表征结构对比提供了新工具。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have achieved remarkable generalization across visual domains, yet little is known about how they internally represent the structure of the world. To address this gap, we use Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) to read concept circuits from ViTs: directed graphs whose nodes correspond to sparse, interpretable concepts and edges capture concept interactions across layers. Our method yields two complementary views of model behavior. The global concept circuit is input-invariant and can be recovered directly from learned cross-layer weights, exposing the reusable "world knowledge" encoded in the model. The instance concept circuit is input-dependent and identifies the concepts and pathways actually used for a specific prediction, enabling faithful example-level explanations. We demonstrate the utility of concept circuits in three ways: (1) Automatic spurious correlation discovery: leveraging the statistics of our global concept circuits to identify shortcut dependencies within the model. (2) Spurious correlation removal: intervening on the instance concept circuit to steer the model towards correct predictions. Empirical results show that our method outperforms existing counterparts by 11.0% on the Waterbird dataset. (3) Model comparison: contrasting the global concept circuits of different foundation models (e.g., CLIP vs. DINO) to reveal how supervision paradigms shape representational structure. Our code is available at https://github.com/deep-real/VisionCLT

</details>


### [296] [GoDeep: Annotation-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding via Language-Space Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09082)
*Thodoris Betsas,Anastasios Doulamis,Andreas Georgopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GoDeep方法，使用视觉-语言模型仅作为翻译器，将每个带姿态图像生成结构化、实体级别的文本描述，然后直接在纯语言嵌入空间中接地、投影和聚合，实现开放词汇3D语义分割，无需任何3D训练语料库或专用3D编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D语义分割方法通常将CLIP特征提升到3D，但CLIP嵌入在组合任务上表现类似词袋模型；且无标注变体往往需要大规模3D训练语料和针对每个领域的专用3D编码器，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 1) 使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）仅作为翻译器，生成结构化、实体级别的3D图像描述；2) 将描述接地、投影并直接在通用纯语言嵌入空间中聚合；3) 无需3D训练语料库或专用3D编码器；4) 提出利用启发式权重（偏向精确而非频繁观察）与可解释性的聚合策略，优化细粒度元素定位；5) 利用语言空间中文本表示的可解释性实现逐点的预测解释。

Result: 在ScanNet++上，GoDeep与在ScanNet上训练的强基线方法相当；在一个5栋建筑的文化遗产基准上，原始分数CLIP变体略优，但一次系统性词汇校正即可逆转排名（第二次独立校正在另一类别上验证了该效应），表明语言空间嵌入更忠实反映物理内容。在真正词表外（OOV）物体上，语言空间嵌入比CLIP嵌入更清晰地区分存在与不存在的物体，且无需任何2D-3D标注即可定位。

Conclusion: 语言空间嵌入相比CLIP嵌入能更忠实地追踪物理内容，并显著提升词表外物体分离能力；由于所有表示均为离散文本，预测可在点级别解释，同时提出的启发式加权与可解释聚合策略可改善细粒度元素定位性能。

Abstract: Open vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation methods typically lift CLIP features into 3D. This embeds points in a joint vision-language space known to behave like a bag-of-words on compositional tasks. Furthermore, even annotation free variants often require a large 3D training corpus and a dedicated 3D encoder per domain. Instead we use a vision-language model purely as a translator. It produces structured, entity-level descriptions of each posed image. These descriptions are grounded, projected, and aggregated directly in a general-purpose, language-only embedding space, with no 3D training corpus or encoder required. On ScanNet++, our pipeline is competitive with strong annotation free baselines trained on ScanNet. On a 5-building cultural heritage benchmark, raw scores initially favor a CLIP-based variant, but a single systematic vocabulary correction reverses this ranking. An effect confirmed by a second, independent correction on a different class, indicating that language-space embeddings track physical content more faithfully. This fidelity extends to genuinely out-of-vocabulary (OOV) objects on ScanNet++ proving that language-space embeddings separate presence from absence objects far more sharply than CLIP-based embeddings do. GoDeep also localize these OOV objects within the scene, all without any 2D-3D annotation. Because every representation remains discrete text, predictions are also explainable at the point level. Finally, exploiting both a heuristic weighting, that favors precise over merely frequent observations and GoDeep's explainability property, we propose an aggregation strategy, as a proof of concept, that favors finer elements localization.

</details>


### [297] [Mask Forcing: Improving Autoregressive Video Diffusion Distillation via Dual-Noise Masking Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09123)
*Zhuoran Zhao,Shengju Qian,Tongtong Liang,Xianghao Kong,Songchun Zhang,Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Xin Wang,Pan Hui,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask Forcing，一种双噪声掩码滚动策略，通过在自回归视频扩散模型的自我滚动过程中注入掩码扰动，缓解反向KL散度导致的模式坍塌问题，从而提升生成视频的视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在蒸馏过程中使用反向KL目标（DMD），存在模式寻求行为，导致学生分布塌缩到教师分布的少数模式上，生成视频出现过饱和和过平滑问题，视觉质量和真实感受限。

Method: 提出Mask Forcing（双噪声掩码滚动策略）：在AR学生自我滚动过程中，沿空间和时间轴随机掩码，向带噪的滚动输入注入更干净的信号。这种扰动鼓励学生探索教师分布的更多区域，使DMD能提供超越学生已覆盖模式的学习信号；同时更干净的token作为去噪指导帮助其他噪声token，减少误差积累。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能提升多种AR视频扩散蒸馏方法的视觉质量，且无需真实视频数据或额外的后训练阶段，效率较高。

Conclusion: Mask Forcing通过简单的掩码扰动策略有效解决了AR视频扩散蒸馏中反向KL导致的模式坍塌问题，是一种通用且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models have shown great potential in real-time video generation. Recent methods distill pretrained bidirectional video diffusion models into causal AR students through Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD), but the generated videos often suffer from over-saturation and over-smoothing issues, resulting in limited visual quality and realism. The key contributing factor is the mode-seeking behavior of the reverse KL objective in DMD, which can cause the student distribution to collapse onto only a few modes of the teacher distribution. To address this, we propose Mask Forcing, a Dual-Noise Masking Rollout strategy that perturbs the AR student self-rollout to mitigate mode collapse induced by reverse-KL mode seeking. The core idea is to inject cleaner signals into noisy rollout inputs via random masks along spatial and temporal axes during the self-rollout process of AR diffusion distillation. Such perturbations encourage the student rollouts to explore more regions of the teacher distribution, allowing DMD to provide learning signals beyond the modes already covered by the student. Moreover, the cleaner tokens act as denoising guidance for other noisier tokens, improving the intermediate rollout predictions and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method improves multiple AR video diffusion distillation methods with higher visual quality efficiently, without incorporating real video data or additional post-training stages.

</details>


### [298] [Canonical Color as a Lens into Concept Decodability in Vision Encoders and VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09124)
*Xiaofu Chen,Stella Frank,Yova Kementchedjhieva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉编码器中是否包含概念性（而非仅直接可见的）信息，以典型颜色（canonical color）作为受控测试案例。通过构建具有典型颜色的物体数据集，使用彩色和灰度图像探测视觉编码器，发现典型颜色可以从灰度图像中解码，且与预测的物体身份相关，表明存在概念性联系。进一步分析完整VLM发现，后训练对视觉编码器的颜色可解码性有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解视觉编码器在视觉语言模型中表征的信息程度——具体来说是编码器中是否包含超越像素层面、具有概念性质的语义信息，而典型颜色（即使物体以灰度呈现，人仍能联想到其典型颜色）是理想的可控测试案例。

Method: 构建包含典型颜色物体的数据集，使用线性探测（linear probing）方法从视觉编码器中解码颜色和物体身份信息，分别使用彩色和灰度图像进行测试；随后将分析扩展到完整的VLM以考察后训练的影响。

Result: 典型颜色可以从灰度图像中线性解码出来，且与预测的物体身份相关联，说明视觉编码器中存在概念性的典型颜色信息；VLM后训练对编码器的颜色可解码性有显著影响。

Conclusion: 典型颜色是追踪视觉编码器和VLM中物体级概念语义信息的有用且可控的视角，揭示了视觉表征中概念性信息的可访问性及其受训练方式影响的重要特性。

Abstract: Visual encoders construct a representation of the image input for Vision-Language models. How much conceptual, as opposed to immediately visible, information does this representation contain? We use canonical color as a controlled test case to ask whether vision encoders make canonical-color information linearly accessible, even when color is removed from the input image. We construct a dataset of objects with canonical colors, and probe vision encoders for both color and object identity using color and grayscale images. We find that canonical color remains decodable from grayscale images, and is tied to predicted object identity, indicating a conceptual link. Extending this analysis to full VLMs, we find that VLM post-training can have a surprisingly large effect on color decodability in the vision encoder. Overall, canonical color provides a usefully controllable lens for tracing object-level conceptual semantic information in vision encoders and VLMs.

</details>


### [299] [Studying Image Tokenizers as Visual Languages in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09143)
*Siting Li,Zhengyang Wang,Simon Shaolei Du,Xi Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个纯自回归测试平台，通过追踪多模态持续预训练中文本、图像、T2I和I2T的任务特定验证损失，系统研究了图像tokenizer作为"视觉语言"在多模态联合建模中的行为，发现损失需按任务分析、T2I与I2T损失与下游性能的关系取决于预测token空间，并揭示更好的重建并不必然带来更低的损失或更强的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像tokenizer研究通常通过孤立指标或仅针对生成/理解任务进行评估，未能充分捕捉视觉token与文本联合建模时的真实行为。

Method: 构建受控的纯自回归测试平台，在多模态持续预训练中同时覆盖文本、图像、T2I和I2T四种预测任务，通过追踪任务特定验证损失来分析损失扩展行为、损失与下游性能的关系，并以三个tokenizer设计维度（判别器、语义监督、词汇量）作为案例研究。

Result: 发现四点关键结果：(1) 损失需按任务分别分析，不同任务有不同扩展行为且对tokenizer排序不同；(2) T2I损失与生成质量的关系受图像token空间影响，而I2T损失在共享文本词表上提供更一致的信号，且与生成和理解性能均相关；(3) 更好的重建并不必然带来更低的损失或更强的下游性能；(4) tokenizer选择可影响联合优化下的文本建模。

Conclusion: 该测试平台为图像tokenizer作为视觉语言的研究提供了互补视角，揭示了其在多模态联合训练中与文本的相互作用，并表明任务特定损失分析比单一重建指标更能指导tokenizer设计与多模态联合建模优化。

Abstract: Image tokenizers define the ``visual language'' of unified multimodal models, yet are commonly studied through isolated metrics or generation-/understanding-only evaluations. These evaluations do not fully capture how visual tokens behave when modeled jointly with text. We build a controlled pure-autoregressive testbed and track task-specific validation losses during multimodal continual pretraining across text, image, text-to-image (T2I), and image-to-text (I2T) prediction. We examine how these losses scale and relate to downstream performance, then use them to study multimodal learnability---how well image and text tokens are jointly modeled---and tokenizer design. We find that (1) losses should be analyzed by task, since they exhibit distinct scaling behavior and rank tokenizers differently. (2) The loss--performance relationship depends on the predicted token space: for a fixed tokenizer, T2I and I2T losses correlate with generation quality, but across tokenizers, the T2I loss--performance relationship shifts with the image-token space, whereas I2T loss, computed over a shared text vocabulary, provides a more consistent signal. I2T loss also correlates with both generation and visual understanding performance after supervised finetuning. Using losses as a lens, we show that (3) better reconstruction does not necessarily yield lower task-specific losses or stronger downstream performance, and that (4) image tokenizer choice can affect text modeling under joint optimization. As case studies, we revisit three tokenizer design axes---the discriminator, semantic supervision, and vocabulary size---to examine their effects on joint modeling and downstream performance. Together, our testbed offers a complementary perspective on image tokenizers as visual languages, highlighting their interplay with text in joint multimodal training.

</details>


### [300] [Point4D: Long-range 4D Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09145)
*Minsik Jeon,Jay Karhade,Deva Ramanan,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Point4D是一种用于长视频序列4D重建的前馈模型，能够在数百帧视频中可靠地推断稠密的逐点3D轨迹，突破了现有4D方法仅能处理几十帧短输入的限制。其关键创新在于灵活的3D查询运动解码器，将轨迹预测与图像平面可见性解耦，并通过跨帧视觉描述符重用提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的4D重建/跟踪方法受限于短输入窗口（最多几十帧），无法处理跨数百帧的长视频序列，需要在长范围动态场景重建方面取得突破。

Method: 1) 提出灵活的3D查询运动解码器，实现轨迹预测与图像平面可见性的解耦；2) 预测的3D端点直接用于下一分块的重新查询，无需重投影或匹配；3) 从任意可见帧提取并重用视觉描述符代替仅依赖源补丁，以提升性能。

Result: Point4D在多个涵盖200帧以上的长视频跟踪基准上取得了最先进的性能，并大幅优于此前的前馈4D方法。

Conclusion: Point4D通过创新的3D查询解码机制和跨帧描述符重用策略，有效解决了长视频序列中的4D重建难题，为数百帧尺度的动态场景理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce Point4D, a feed-forward model for 4D reconstruction of long-range video sequences. Point4D is able to reliably infer dense per-point 3D trajectories across multi-hundred-frame videos, unlike existing 4D methods that are limited to short input windows of at most a few dozen frames. A key innovation that enables this is our flexible 3D query-based motion decoder that decouples trajectory prediction from image-plane visibility. The predicted 3D endpoints are then directly re-queried in the next chunk without re-projection or matching. Furthermore, we show that extracting and reusing a visual descriptor from an arbitrary frame where the point is visible leads to better performance than relying solely on the source patch. Overall, Point4D achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-video tracking benchmarks spanning over 200 frames and largely outperforms previous feed-forward 4D method. Project page: https://point-4d.github.io

</details>


### [301] [SyncWorld: Visual Calibration Enables World Models as Zero-Shot Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09155)
*Yuncong Yang,Zhengtao Han,Furkan Ozyurt,Zeyuan Yang,Han Yang,Junyi Cao,Haoyu Zhen,Yilun Du,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SyncWorld，一种动作条件世界模型，通过视觉校准片段（配对的帧和动作）在上下文中指定特定设置的"动作-视觉映射"，实现无需额外训练的零样本跨环境模拟器，并可支持测试时策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域的世界模型在扩展时面临关键障碍：动作在像素空间中并非通用语言——视觉环境、相机视角、机器人位置或本体的变化都会改变相同数值动作的视觉表现，导致混合训练下产生冲突监督和部署时的脆弱泛化。现有方法缺乏对新环境中低层机器人动作的细粒度可控模拟能力。

Method: SyncWorld利用视觉校准片段（展示所有可控自由度的配对帧和动作）在上下文中指定特定设置的"动作-视觉映射"。训练中结合视觉校准上下文，教会模型通过视觉证据解释动作，并在显式校准不可用时利用交互历史。

Result: 实验表明，SyncWorld能够在未见过的设置中准确模拟动作结果，并且其模拟推送能力支持无需训练的测试时策略改进。

Conclusion: SyncWorld通过上下文中的视觉校准机制解决了动作在不同环境中的视觉表现不一致问题，实现了零样本的跨环境动作模拟，为机器人世界模型的通用化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used as policy-in-the-loop imagination environments, where reliable rollouts require fine-grained controllability with respect to low-level robot actions. A key obstacle to scaling such models in robotics is that actions are not a universal language in pixel space: changes in visual environment, camera view, robot placement, or embodiment alter how the same numerical action manifests visually, leading to conflicting supervision under mixed training and brittle generalization at deployment. We introduce SyncWorld, an action-conditioned world model that serves as a zero-shot simulator across unseen environments without any additional training. SyncWorld leverages a visual calibration episode---paired frames and actions that showcase all the controllable degrees of freedom---to specify the setup-specific Action--Visual Mapping in context. Training with visual calibration contexts teaches the model to interpret actions through visual evidence and to leverage interaction history when explicit calibration is unavailable. Experiments show that SyncWorld can accurately simulate action outcomes in previously unseen settings, and that its capability of simulating rollouts enables test-time policy improvement without training.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [302] [Observability Analysis of Joint Steering and Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05498)
*Subodh Mishra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对平面自行车模型车辆，联合估计车辆位姿、平面LiDAR外参标定和转向角偏差，分析了不同运动模式下的局部弱可观测性。研究发现静止和单一运动模式存在不可观方向，而直线与圆弧组合运动可消除退化，实现七状态系统的完全局部弱可观测，为标定轨迹选取提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶中，车辆位姿、LiDAR外参标定与转向偏差的联合估计对定位和感知精度至关重要，但目前缺乏对联合估计系统在不同运动模式下可观测性的严格理论分析，难以指导标定轨迹设计。

Method: 采用基于李导数的非线性可观测性分析方法，针对静止、直线、恒曲率和直线加圆弧组合运动四种工况，构建可观测性矩阵并分析其零空间，考察七状态系统（含位姿、LiDAR平移与偏航偏移、转向偏差）的可观测性退化情况。

Result: 静止运动和单一运动基元均保留不可观方向，存在退化；而直线与曲线组合的运动方式能够消除所有被识别的退化问题，使得七状态系统达到完全局部弱可观测。

Conclusion: 该分析为选择能充分激励转向和传感器外参参数的标定轨迹提供了理论基础，直线加圆弧组合运动的轨迹是确保联合估计系统可观测性的有效选择。

Abstract: This technical report studies the local weak observability of a planar bicycle-model vehicle when vehicle pose, planar LiDAR extrinsic calibration, and steering-angle bias are estimated jointly. A Lie-derivative-based nonlinear observability analysis is used to examine stationary, straight-line, constant-curvature, and combined straight-plus-arc motion. The resulting observability matrices and nullspaces describe how pose, LiDAR translation and yaw offsets, and steering bias become coupled under different motion primitives. Stationary motion and individual motion primitives retain unobservable directions, whereas the combination of straight and curved motion removes the identified degeneracies and yields full local weak observability of the seven-state system. The analysis provides a theoretical basis for selecting calibration trajectories that sufficiently excite both steering and sensor-extrinsic parameters.

</details>


### [303] [Multi-robot Learning-based Informative Path Planning Using Spatio-Temporal Gaussian Process Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05515)
*Muqing Cao,Yunwoo Lee,Junbin Yuan,Lorenzo Schenk,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于栅格化时空高斯过程-卡尔曼滤波框架的学习型多机器人信息路径规划（IPP）方法，用于持续目标监测。该方法将匿名目标存在表示为单一潜在场，支持任意视野和距离相关噪声，并通过对角协方差交集实现分散式信念融合，最后结合强化学习策略进行图邻接选择。仿真结果显示平均目标不确定性降低约20%，并在两架无人机的实地实验中验证了超7000平方米户外搜索场景的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人信息路径规划方法在处理持续目标监测时存在两方面局限：一是通常依赖简化的感知模型，二是依赖集中式的信念更新，无法适应实际传感与通信约束下的分散式部署需求。

Method: 提出栅格化的时空GP-Kalman滤波框架：将匿名目标存在建模为单一离散网格上的潜在场；递归更新考虑相机足迹内所有可见栅格，支持任意视野和距离相关噪声；采用GP一致的时间过程更新，通过随时间的方差膨胀处理移动目标和信息过时；分散式部署中每个机器人维护独立mapper并交换压缩信念摘要，使用对角协方差交集融合以保持未知互相关下的保守性；将mapper与基于强化学习的图邻接选择策略集成。

Result: 仿真基准测试显示相比学习型方法和经典拍卖/覆盖基线，平均目标不确定性降低约20%，目标访问率提升；真实世界两架无人机实验验证了在超过7000平方米的大型户外场地中的迁移可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过统一的潜在场表征、支持任意传感模型、分散式保守信念融合及学习型规划策略的结合，有效提升了多机器人持久目标监测的性能与实用性，并验证了从仿真到真实场景的良好迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-robot informative path planning (IPP) for persistent target monitoring requires robots to reason about spatial uncertainty, temporal evolution, and practical sensing and communication constraints. Recent learning-based multi-robot IPP methods use Gaussian Processes (GPs) for target uncertainty, but often rely on simplified sensing models and centralized belief updates. We propose a grid-based spatio-temporal GP-Kalman filtering framework for learning-based multi-robot IPP. Instead of maintaining one GP per target, we represent anonymous target presence as a single latent field over a discrete workspace grid. The proposed recursive update considers all visible cells inside a camera footprint and supports arbitrary fields of view and range-dependent noise. A GP-consistent temporal process update accounts for moving targets and stale information by inflating uncertainty over time. For decentralized deployment, each robot maintains its own mapper and exchanges compact belief summaries rather than raw measurements. Received beliefs are fused using diagonal covariance intersection to remain conservative under unknown inter-robot correlations. We integrate the mapper with a reinforcement-learning policy for graph-based neighbor selection. Simulation benchmarks show about 20% lower average target uncertainty and improved target visitation compared with learning-based and classical auction/coverage baselines. Real-world two-UAV experiments demonstrate transfer to outdoor multi-robot search over a large field of more than 7000 square meters.

</details>


### [304] [Robots Influencing Humans to Reveal their Goals during Collaboration and Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05519)
*Debasmita Ghose,Oz Gitelson,Michal Lewkowicz,Jake Brawer,Marynel Vazquez,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一策略，通过在关键决策点(CDPs)驱动人类行为以加速人机交互中的目标推断，并在烹饪任务和捉迷藏游戏中验证了其有效性与及时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中，快速准确推断人类目标是实现高效协作与对抗的关键，但现有方法在信息增益与任务推进之间缺乏有效平衡，导致目标推断速度慢、准确率低。

Method: 核心是利用目标条件策略分歧度量形式化关键决策点(CDPs)，即在竞争性策略产生不同动作的状态处驱动机器人引导人类，并结合退避时域规划器探索未来动作序列，优化同时权衡任务进度与信息增益的成本函数。

Result: 在协作的全可观测烹饪任务和竞争的局部可观测捉迷藏游戏中，分别在仿真和真实机器人上验证，该方法比基线策略在目标推断准确性和时序上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 提出的CDP驱动的统一推断策略可显著提升人机交互中目标推断的速度与准确性，适用于协作与竞争等多种交互场景。

Abstract: We propose a unified strategy for fast goal inference in human-robot interaction. The core idea is to drive the human toward Critical Decision Points (CDPs)-states where competing human strategies prescribe different next actions and thus maximally reveal the goal. We formalise CDPs using a goal-conditioned policy divergence measure and incorporate them into a Receding-Horizon Planner that explores future action sequences while optimizing a cost function balancing task progress and information gain. We evaluate this approach in both a collaborative, fully observable cooking task and a competitive, partially observable hide-and-seek game, each in simulation and on real robots. In both scenarios, our method infers human goals more accurately and earlier than baseline strategies.

</details>


### [305] [Situation Awareness for Intelligent Data Distribution in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05521)
*Falk Dettinger,Akshay Narla,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bird's-Eye-View（鸟瞰图）图像的车载交通情境识别概念，旨在通过识别当前交通情境来优先获取和利用相关数据，从而提高协作感知中传感器数据管理的效率，减少不必要的数据传输量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆受限于车载传感器性能和遮挡盲区，感知质量会下降。协作感知虽能通过V2X通信补充数据，但会带来大量数据传输开销。如果能根据当前交通情境动态选择相关数据，则可显著提升数据管理的效率。

Method: 首先利用目标检测和语义分割识别车辆周围情境，随后通过由开源投影变换网络Cam2BEV和情境识别神经网络组成的情境识别模块，理解交通上下文。在CARLA模拟器上使用内置RGB相机和语义分割相机进行评估验证，并在Cityscapes和nuScenes真实驾驶数据集上验证了该模块的实际可移植性。

Result: 所提出的情境识别方法在CARLA模拟器上完成了概念验证和评估，并在Cityscapes及nuScenes数据集上验证了情境识别模块在真实场景中的可移植性；源代码已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该情境识别方法能够通过根据当前交通情境优先处理相关数据，实现高效的传感器数据管理，有助于减少协作感知中冗余的数据传输。

Abstract: The limitations of on-board sensors and blind spots caused by occlusion cause the reduction of perception quality in autonomous vehicles. In such cases, cooperative perception provides additional data via Vehicle-to-Everything communication to enhance local perception, causing a large volume of data transmission. The vehicle can focus on acquiring and utilizing relevant data according to the prevailing road context by identifying the current traffic situation. To achieve this, we propose a concept for the situation identification of the vehicle using Bird's-Eye-View images. Firstly, the situation around the vehicle is identified using object detection with semantic segmentation, followed by understanding the context of the traffic using a situation identification module consisting of an open-source projective transformation network Cam2BEV and a situation identification neural network. The concept was evaluated and validated by running the software on the CARLA simulator using the in-built RGB camera and the semantic segmentation camera. Additionally, the portability of the situation identification module for real-world applications was verified on Cityscapes and nuScenes urban driving datasets. Overall, the proposed situation identification approach enables efficient sensor data management by prioritizing relevant data to the current traffic situation. The source code is available in the following link: https://github.com/akshaynarla/DySi_Select

</details>


### [306] [Information-Guided Safe Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Gas Source Localization using sUAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05569)
*Sachin Giri,Thomas Zhao,Matthew Huynh,YangQuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信息引导的安全强化学习框架（IGSRL），用于解决小型无人机（sUAS）在湍流大气边界层中自主定位逸散性气体源的病态逆问题。该框架将经典经验可观性Gramian（EMGR）规划器与学习型Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）探索策略相结合，通过确定性元监督器基于KL散度动态混合确定性与探索性决策，并采用Robust Control Barrier Function（RCBF）确保安全，最终在GPU加速的3D仿真环境中实现近80%的定位成功率，远超经典基线（约30%）且零安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在湍流大气边界层中，气体浓度场高度间歇，破坏了经典梯度导航方法的假设，导致数据驱动的估计器受到严重噪声和虚假局部最小值的影响。小型无人机自主定位逸散性气体源本质上是一个病态逆问题，传统方法难以有效应对，因此需要一种能够在高度不确定环境中进行鲁棒、安全源定位的新框架。

Method: 框架分为三个核心部分：（1）EMGR确定性信息规划器，基于经验可观性Gramian驱动源估计；（2）SAC探索策略，通过强化学习提供探索行为，克服确定性规划器的Gramian偏差问题；（3）元监督器，利用KL散度监控估计器可靠性，动态融合确定性利用与学习探索。训练采用渐进式课程学习，并通过严格执行的RCBF保障安全约束。

Result: 在GPU加速的3D仿真环境（耦合Eulerian风场求解器与Lagrangian puff扩散模型）中，所提框架对复杂移动源实现了近80%的定位成功率，比经典基线（约30%）大幅提升，同时实现零安全违规。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将经典控制理论的可观性分析与现代强化学习相结合，能够有效解决湍流环境中气体源定位这一病态逆问题。确定性规划与学习探索的动态混合策略，配合严格的安全屏障，为sUAS在复杂大气环境中的自主气体源追踪提供了一个稳健且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: The autonomous localization of fugitive gas emissions using small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) constitutes a fundamentally ill-posed inverse problem. In turbulent atmospheric boundary layers, highly intermittent scalar concentration fields violate the assumptions of classical gradient-based navigation, causing data-driven estimators to suffer from severe noise and spurious local minima. To address these challenges, we introduce an Information-Guided Safe Reinforcement Learning framework evaluated within a custom, GPU-accelerated 3D simulation environment coupling an Eulerian wind solver with a Lagrangian puff dispersion model. We identify a critical vulnerability in deterministic information-seeking planners - a Gramian bias where agents act greedily upon flawed early estimates, starving the estimator of spatial diversity. To systematically break this degeneracy, our architecture integrates a classical empirical observability Gramian (EMGR) planner with a learned Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) exploratory policy. A deterministic meta-supervisor actively monitors estimator reliability via Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, dynamically blending deterministic exploitation with learned exploration to steer the sUAS into high-information zones. Trained via a progressive curriculum and safeguarded by a strictly enforced Robust Control Barrier Function (RCBF), our RL framework achieves nearly 80% localization success on complex, mobile sources - drastically outperforming classical baselines (~30%) - while ensuring zero safety violations.

</details>


### [307] [Benchmarking Dexterity of Multifingered Robot Hands: A Review and Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05585)
*Anthony Shilati,Anunth Ramaswami,Luke Batteas,Sylvia Tan,Anthony Barcio,Sairam Umakanth,Preksha Rao,David McDougall,Landry Graves,Ahmet A. Ozkan,Yunsoo Yoon,Arushi Pradhan,Michael G. Henry,Rohan Kota,Damian Gonzalez,Gray C. Thomas,Gary K. Fedder,J. Edward Colgate,Kevin M. Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了多指机器人手部灵巧性基准测试的现状，提出了美国国家科学基金会HAND工程研究中心的观点，特别关注精细的手内操作。文章引入了一个由三个基准级别组成的框架，对应逐渐增加的系统复杂度，并在每个级别回顾了代表性基准，同时提出了新的基准和评估指标以弥补现有文献的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器手是AI与物理世界交互的关键接口，多指手相比简单夹爪具有更强的多功能性、精确性和适应性。然而，灵巧性是一个复杂多维的概念，现有基准测试缺乏系统性的框架来评估多指机器手的精细操作能力。

Method: 提出了一个由三个基准级别组成的框架，分别对应逐渐增加的系统复杂度（从单个手部到完整系统集成），并在每个级别回顾了代表性基准，同时提出了新的基准和评估指标来弥补现有文献的局限性。

Result: 该综述建立了多指手灵巧性基准测试的分层框架，识别了现有基准在每个系统复杂度级别上的不足，并提出了新的基准测试方案和评估指标。

Conclusion: 多指机器人手的灵巧性基准测试需要多层次、系统化的框架来全面评估，本文提出的三级基准框架为该领域提供了标准化评估的参考方向，有助于推动物理AI愿景的实现。

Abstract: Robot hands are a key interface between AI and the physical world, making advances in robotic dexterity essential to realizing the vision of physical AI. While impressive dexterity has been demonstrated with simple grippers, multifingered hands offer the potential for substantially greater versatility, precision, and adaptability in manipulation. In this review, we survey the state of the art in benchmarking the dexterity of multifingered robot hands. Recognizing dexterity as a complex and multifaceted concept, we present the perspective of the U.S. National Science Foundation HAND Engineering Research Center, with a particular focus on fine in-hand manipulation. We introduce a framework consisting of three benchmark levels that correspond to increasing system complexity, review representative benchmarks at each level, and propose new benchmarks and metrics to address limitations in the literature. More information can be found at https://hand-erc.github.io/benchmarking/.

</details>


### [308] [GE-Act 2.0: Pretraining and Scaling a World-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05588)
*AgiBot Research Team,Renhang Liu,Wenzhi Zhao,Zhuo Yang,Liliang Chen,Pengfei Zhou,Shengcong Chen,Guanghui Ren,Youlun Peng,Rongjun Jin,Nan Wang,Sukai Wang,Xindong He,Jinyuan Feng,Ziyu Xiong,Linqing Zhong,Yifei Wei,Feng Han,Long Zhang,Da Huang,Nanshu Zhao,Chenghao Yin,Mo Wu,Zhaodong Yan,Kongtao Hu,Yuxiang Yan,Aogelijiang Niyazi,Yu Fang,Jia Zeng,Lizhu Meng,Daizhen Lv,Haoyu Cao,Zhiwen Hou,Lianjin Ye,Yuehan Niu,Zhikai Cai,Xuan Hu,Hui Min,Xiongfeng Cai,Yue Liao,Jing Wu,Soujanya Poria,Ye Li,Sanping Zhou,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GE-Act 2.0世界动作模型，所有可训练组件均从零开始在操作数据上初始化，通过控制导向自编码器、单步视觉规划器和逆动力学模型的组合，以及知识对齐选择性优化策略，在100个任务上达到44.1%的成功率，验证了世界动作模型的预训练与缩放潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型大多继承预训练的视频生成器，导致WAM自身的预训练和缩放规律尚未被充分探索。作者希望从头训练世界动作模型，研究其在机器人操作领域的scaling行为与泛化能力。

Method: GE-Act 2.0由三个从零初始化的组件构成：CoAE在激进压缩下保留动作和指令相关信息；SVP通过单次可微前向传播生成完整未来状态，使视觉规划和逆动力学可在互补数据上分别预训练；随后通过KASO进行联合训练，仅选择与记录动作行为兼容的预测未来帧来减少监督不匹配。

Result: 协同训练数据从300小时扩展至30,000小时后，G1-OP成功率从17.1%提升至44.1%，G2-90D从13.4%提升至31.1%；G2-90D仅占数据不到2%却提升17.7个百分点，表明存在跨具身迁移。增益覆盖19/20和18/20技能组，技能覆盖与零样本OOD成功率显著相关（Pearson r=0.80）。模型在90%以上试验中正确理解物体、颜色、形状和位置引用。

Conclusion: 从零训练的GE-Act 2.0验证了WAM预训练和缩放的有效性：数据规模扩大带来显著性能提升，跨具身迁移可行，且模型不仅能遵循指令，还能在指令与已承诺行为或场景常规冲突时正确响应。

Abstract: World-action models (WAM) predict future states to guide robot actions, enabling learning from both action-free video and action-labeled interaction. Most inherit pretrained video generators, leaving WAM pretraining and scaling underexplored. We introduce Genie Envisioner Act 2.0 (GE-Act 2.0), a world-action model whose trainable generative and action components are all initialized from scratch on manipulation data. It combines a control-oriented autoencoder (CoAE), a single-step visual planner (SVP), and an inverse dynamics model (IDM). CoAE retains action- and instruction-relevant information under aggressive compression, while SVP produces a complete future state in one differentiable pass, so visual planning and inverse dynamics can be pretrained separately on complementary data. The components are then jointly trained with knowledge-aligned selective optimization (KASO), which reduces mismatched supervision by selecting only predicted futures judged behaviorally compatible with the recorded action. We evaluate pretrained checkpoints directly, without per-task fine-tuning, on 100 tasks across 20 manipulation skill groups with held-out scenes, backgrounds, lighting, and object instances. Scaling co-training data from 300 to 30,000 hours raises success from 17.1% to 44.1% on G1-OP and from 13.4% to 31.1% on G2-90D; despite comprising less than 2% of the co-training data, G2-90D improves by 17.7 points, suggesting cross-embodiment transfer. Gains span 19/20 and 18/20 skill groups, and skill-specific coverage strongly correlates with zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) success (Pearson r=0.80; Spearman rho=0.85). Under the same protocol, the model grounds object, color, shape, and position references in at least 90% of trials and follows explicit instructions even when they conflict with an already-committed behavior or a conventional scene association.

</details>


### [309] [Adaptive Cost-Sensitive Machine Learning for Autonomous Robot Navigation Failure Prediction: When Not All Errors Are Equal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05593)
*Rifa Ferzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将自主机器人导航失败预测重构为"后果敏感型预测"，通过结合严重性、速度、障碍物接近度和感知不确定性的状态依赖后果函数来调制训练权重，在2000个模拟差分驱动回合和UCI SCITOS G5外部数据集上验证，显著提升高严重性/碰撞召回率并降低遗漏后果成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人导航失败不仅类别严重性不同，其物理发生情境也各异——低速可靠感知下的险情不等于高速、近距离障碍或感知退化下的同类事件。现有预测方法未将物理情境纳入后果考量，需要一种能够区分导航失败物理后果的预测框架。

Method: 首先建立固定基线，通过分类严重性调制训练权重；随后引入自适应扩展，定义状态依赖后果函数，综合严重性、归一化速度、障碍物接近度及感知不确定性，并加入随条件恶化而上升的风险敏感项。采用episode级GroupKFold在2,000个模拟回合（约1,000,000时间步）上评估，并以UCI SCITOS G5数据集做外部验证；另设受控扩展，在600回合中令108回合终止于接触。

Result: 固定权重将Logistic回归高严重性召回率从0.851提升至0.985，遗漏后果成本从1,940降至313；自适应扩展达到0.998和82。但在匹配假阳性条件下判别优势有限（0.986对0.984），收益主要来自更保守的工作点。碰撞扩展中碰撞召回率从0.850升至0.966（固定）和0.984（自适应），遗漏碰撞成本从1,000降至105。效果在五折交叉验证和三倍上下文系数范围内均一致稳定。

Conclusion: 上下文相关的后果建模为按物理风险分配保守性提供了原则性机制。但需要注意：在匹配假阳性条件下判别性提升有限，主要增益源于更保守的操作点选择而非排序能力改进，且高假阳性率（0.799）可能限制实际部署价值。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation failures differ not only in categorical severity but also in the physical context in which they occur. A near-miss at low speed under reliable sensing is not equivalent to the same event during rapid motion, close obstacle approach or degraded perception. This paper reframes navigation failure prediction as consequence-sensitive forecasting. We first establish a fixed baseline in which training weights are modulated by categorical severity, then introduce an adaptive extension defining a state-dependent consequence function combining severity with normalised velocity, obstacle proximity and sensing uncertainty, together with a risk-sensitivity term that rises as conditions deteriorate. We evaluate on 2,000 simulated differential-drive episodes (~1,000,000 timesteps) using episode-level GroupKFold, with external validation on the UCI SCITOS G5 dataset. Fixed weighting raises Logistic Regression high-severity recall from 0.851 to 0.985 and reduces missed consequence cost from 1,940 to 313; the adaptive extension reaches 0.998 and 82. Under matched false-positive conditions, however, the discriminative advantage is modest (0.986 versus 0.984), so most of the gain reflects a more conservative operating point rather than better ranking. The effect is consistent across all five folds and stable across a threefold span of context coefficients. Because the primary simulation produced no collisions, we add a controlled extension in which 108 of 600 episodes terminate in contact: collision recall rises from 0.850 to 0.966 (fixed) and 0.984 (adaptive), with missed collision cost falling from 1,000 to 105, at false-positive rates of 0.413 and 0.799, respectively. Context-dependent consequence modelling thus provides a principled mechanism for allocating conservatism by physical risk.

</details>


### [310] [Closed-Loop Evaluation of Bird's-Eye-View Maps from Cross-View Transformers as Inputs to Behavior-Cloning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05783)
*Felipe Carlos dos Santos,Eric Antonelo,Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶中BEV表征从仿真（真值）迁移到现实（相机预测）时的感知误差对闭环驾驶性能的影响，提出六通道BEV表示和KDE加权损失方案，发现全局IoU不能代表驾驶性能，关键位置预测质量与路线通道才是导航成败的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真中可用的真值BEV图在现实世界需由相机预测替代，但预测引入的感知误差对闭环驾驶性能的下游影响尚不明确，需要系统研究这一差距。
